# PDF Analyser v0.3.0 - Full Source
Generated from the release tree. Binary fixtures and runtime output are omitted.


===== FILE: .env.example =====

# Project and ports
COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=pdf-analyser
APP_VERSION=0.3.0
TOOLS_BIND_HOST=127.0.0.1
TOOLS_PORT=8090
AGENT_BIND_HOST=127.0.0.1
AGENT_PORT=8100
GROBID_BIND_HOST=127.0.0.1
GROBID_PORT=8086
GROBID_ADMIN_PORT=8085

# Host output location. Relative paths are resolved from this project directory.
# Linux example: /srv/pdf-results
# macOS example: /Users/amir/pdf-results
# Windows Docker Desktop example: C:/Users/amir/pdf-results
OUTPUT_DIR=./outputs

# Linux ownership for files written into OUTPUT_DIR.
# install.sh fills these automatically. Docker Desktop users can keep 1000/1000.
HOST_UID=1000
HOST_GID=1000

# Upload and worker limits
MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB=100
MAX_CONCURRENCY=1
LOG_LEVEL=INFO

# Extraction
DOCLING_MAX_NUM_PAGES=300
DOCLING_MAX_CONCURRENCY=1
DOCLING_DOCUMENT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=1800
DOCLING_ACCELERATOR_NUM_THREADS=4
DOCLING_PRELOAD_ON_STARTUP=true
GROBID_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=300
GROBID_MAX_RETRIES=2

# Canonical output
KEEP_RAW_EXTRACTOR_RESPONSE=false
OVERWRITE_EXISTING_PACKAGE=false
CREATE_ZIP_PACKAGE=true

# Ollama vision enrichment
LLM_ENABLED=true
LLM_DESCRIBE_ALL_ELEMENTS=true
LLM_MAX_ITEMS_PER_DOCUMENT=500
OLLAMA_MODEL=qwen2.5vl:3b
OLLAMA_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=600
OLLAMA_SERVER_KEEP_ALIVE=0
OLLAMA_REQUEST_KEEP_ALIVE=0
OLLAMA_TEMPERATURE=0.0

# Quality
QUALITY_GREEN_THRESHOLD=0.90
QUALITY_YELLOW_THRESHOLD=0.65

        patterns: ["*"]

===== FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml =====

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, master]
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  agent:
    name: Agent lint and tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: agent
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: agent/requirements-dev.txt
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: python -m pip install --upgrade pip && pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
      - name: Check formatting
        run: ruff format --check app tests
      - name: Lint
        run: ruff check app tests
      - name: Unit tests
        run: pytest -q
      - name: Compile Python
        run: python -m compileall -q app

  tools:
    name: Tools lint and tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: tools/api
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: |
            tools/api/requirements-base.txt
            tools/api/requirements-dev.txt
      - name: Install lightweight test dependencies
        run: python -m pip install --upgrade pip && pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
      - name: Check formatting
        run: ruff format --check .
      - name: Lint
        run: ruff check .
      - name: Unit tests
        run: pytest -q
      - name: Compile Python
        run: python -m compileall -q app

  compose:
    name: Compose validation
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Create environment files
        run: |
          cp tools/.env.example tools/.env
          cp agent/.env.example agent/.env
      - name: Validate tools CPU Compose
        working-directory: tools
        run: docker compose -f docker-compose.yml config --quiet
      - name: Validate tools GPU Compose
        working-directory: tools
        run: docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.gpu.yml config --quiet
      - name: Validate agent Compose
        working-directory: agent
        run: docker compose -f docker-compose.yml config --quiet
      - name: Check shell scripts
        run: |
          bash -n tools/scripts/check_gpu.sh
          bash -n tools/scripts/smoke_test.sh
          sh -n agent/docker-entrypoint.sh


===== FILE: .github/workflows/docker-build.yml =====

name: Docker Build

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, master]
    paths:
      - "agent/**"
      - ".github/workflows/docker-build.yml"
  pull_request:
    paths:
      - "agent/**"
      - ".github/workflows/docker-build.yml"
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  agent-image:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3
      - name: Build agent image
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: ./agent
          file: ./agent/Dockerfile
          push: false
          tags: pdf-canonical-agent:ci
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max


===== FILE: .github/workflows/integration.yml =====

name: Full Stack Integration

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      tools_mode:
        description: "Run tools in cpu or gpu mode"
        required: true
        type: choice
        options: [cpu, gpu]
        default: cpu

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  smoke:
    # Register a self-hosted runner with these labels. The full tools stack is
    # intentionally not started on normal GitHub-hosted PR runners because it
    # downloads large Docling/GROBID model images and may require an NVIDIA GPU.
    runs-on: [self-hosted, linux, x64, pdf-analyser]
    timeout-minutes: 90
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Initialize repository
        run: make init
      - name: Start stack
        run: |
          if [ "${{ inputs.tools_mode }}" = "gpu" ]; then
            make up-gpu
          else
            make up-cpu
          fi
      - name: Wait for APIs
        run: |
          python tools/scripts/wait_for_url.py http://localhost:8090/health/ready --timeout 3600
          python tools/scripts/wait_for_url.py http://localhost:8100/health/ready --timeout 300
      - name: Process fixture
        run: |
          curl --fail --show-error \
            -X POST http://localhost:8100/v1/process \
            -F "file=@tools/tests/fixtures/scientific_sample.pdf;type=application/pdf" \
            -F "use_llm=false" \
            -o agent-result.json
          jq . agent-result.json
      - name: Validate package
        run: |
          DOCUMENT_ID=$(jq -r .document_id agent-result.json)
          test -f "agent/data/outputs/${DOCUMENT_ID}/canonical_document.json"
          test -f "agent/data/outputs/${DOCUMENT_ID}/source/original.pdf"
          jq -e '.quality.status | IN("green", "yellow", "red")' \
            "agent/data/outputs/${DOCUMENT_ID}/canonical_document.json"
      - name: Upload canonical package
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: canonical-package
          path: |
            agent-result.json
            agent/data/outputs/*.zip
            agent/data/outputs/*/canonical_document.json
      - name: Logs on failure
        if: failure()
        run: |
          (cd tools && docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.gpu.yml logs --tail=500) || true
          (cd agent && docker compose logs --tail=500) || true
      - name: Stop stack
        if: always()
        run: |
          make down-gpu || make down || true

===== FILE: CONTRIBUTING.md =====

# Contributing

## Development setup

```bash
cd agent
python3.12 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
make check
```

For tools development:

```bash
cd tools/api
python3.12 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
ruff format --check .
ruff check .
pytest -q
```

## Pull requests

- Keep deterministic extraction/validation separate from LLM-derived data.
- Never overwrite parser evidence with an LLM result.
- Add tests for schema or merge changes.
- Update the README when configuration, commands, or output paths change.
- Do not commit PDFs containing private or licensed content.
- Do not commit `.env`, model caches, generated assets, or output packages.

## Code quality

Root CI requires:

```bash
make check
```

Use focused commits and describe any output-schema changes explicitly.


===== FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md =====

# Distribution and release guide

## Recommended release contents

Ship these files in every tagged release:

```text
pdf-analyser/
├── compose.yaml
├── compose.nvidia.yaml
├── .env.example
├── scripts/
│   ├── install.sh
│   ├── install-nvidia.sh
│   └── install.ps1
├── tools/
├── agent/
├── README.md
└── DEPLOYMENT.md
```

A user can then unzip the release and run one installer script.

## Source-build release

This repository is immediately usable as a source-build release:

```bash
./scripts/install.sh
```

Docker builds `extractor-api` and `agent-api` locally. This is easy to publish but the first installation takes longer.

## Prebuilt-image release

For the easiest end-user installation, publish versioned application images, for example:

```text
ghcr.io/OWNER/pdf-analyser-extractor:0.3.0-cpu
ghcr.io/OWNER/pdf-analyser-extractor:0.3.0-cu126
ghcr.io/OWNER/pdf-analyser-agent:0.3.0
```

Then create a release Compose file containing only `image:` references. Pin application versions; do not depend on `latest` for your own images.

The CPU images can be published for more than one architecture only when every base image and binary dependency supports those architectures. Validate `linux/amd64` first, then add `linux/arm64` after full integration testing.

## Output contract

There are two independent output controls:

1. `OUTPUT_DIR` in `.env`: host parent directory.
2. `output_folder` in each API request: child package name.

Example:

```dotenv
OUTPUT_DIR=/srv/research-results
```

```bash
-F "output_folder=paper_2026_001"
```

Final host path:

```text
/srv/research-results/paper_2026_001/
```

The API never accepts an arbitrary output path from a request. This prevents a caller from writing outside the administrator-selected output root.

## Production hardening

Before exposing the service beyond localhost:

- place a reverse proxy in front of the Agent API;
- enable TLS;
- require an API key or identity-aware authentication;
- configure request-body size and rate limits;
- keep GROBID, Ollama, and the Tools API private unless external access is required;
- run vulnerability scans on released images;
- pin dependency versions and image tags;
- back up the selected `OUTPUT_DIR` and named model volumes as appropriate.


===== FILE: Makefile =====

SHELL := /bin/bash

COMPOSE := docker compose -f compose.yaml
COMPOSE_GPU := docker compose -f compose.yaml -f compose.nvidia.yaml

.PHONY: init up-cpu up-gpu ready process logs logs-gpu down down-gpu pull-model test lint format check clean

init:
	@test -f .env || cp .env.example .env
	@mkdir -p outputs
	@echo "Edit .env if needed, especially OUTPUT_DIR and OLLAMA_MODEL."

up-cpu: init
	$(COMPOSE) up -d --build

up-gpu: init
	$(COMPOSE_GPU) up -d --build

ready:
	@echo "Tools:" && curl -fsS http://localhost:8090/health/ready | jq
	@echo "Agent:" && curl -fsS http://localhost:8100/health/ready | jq
	@echo "Runtime:" && curl -fsS http://localhost:8100/v1/runtime | jq

process:
	@test -n "$(PDF)" || (echo "Usage: make process PDF=/absolute/path/article.pdf [OUTPUT_FOLDER=case_001] [USE_LLM=true] [OVERWRITE=false]" && exit 2)
	@args=( \
	  --fail --show-error -X POST http://localhost:8100/v1/process \
	  -F "file=@$(PDF);type=application/pdf" \
	  -F "use_llm=$(if $(USE_LLM),$(USE_LLM),false)" \
	); \
	if [[ -n "$(OUTPUT_FOLDER)" ]]; then args+=( -F "output_folder=$(OUTPUT_FOLDER)" ); fi; \
	if [[ -n "$(OVERWRITE)" ]]; then args+=( -F "overwrite=$(OVERWRITE)" ); fi; \
	curl "$${args[@]}" -o agent-result.json
	jq . agent-result.json

pull-model:
	$(COMPOSE) run --rm ollama-model-init

logs:
	$(COMPOSE) logs -f --tail=100

logs-gpu:
	$(COMPOSE_GPU) logs -f --tail=100

down:
	$(COMPOSE) down --remove-orphans

down-gpu:
	$(COMPOSE_GPU) down --remove-orphans

test:
	$(MAKE) -C tools test
	$(MAKE) -C agent test

lint:
	$(MAKE) -C tools lint
	$(MAKE) -C agent lint

format:
	$(MAKE) -C tools format
	$(MAKE) -C agent format

check:
	$(MAKE) -C agent check
	cd tools/api && ruff format --check . && ruff check . && python -m pytest -q && python -m compileall -q app

clean:
	-$(COMPOSE) down --remove-orphans
	rm -rf outputs/*
	rm -f agent-result.json


===== FILE: README.md =====

# PDF Analyser v0.3.0

A Docker-first scientific PDF understanding stack:

```text
PDF
  -> Docling + GROBID extraction
  -> hierarchical sections/subsections
  -> canonical JSON and exported assets
  -> optional Ollama vision descriptions for figures, tables, and formulas
```

The root Compose stack starts all services on one private Docker network:

```text
grobid -> extractor-api -> agent-api
                    \-> ollama
```

The Agent writes complete packages into a host directory selected in `.env`.

## Fastest installation

### Linux or macOS, CPU mode

```bash
unzip pdf-analyser-v0.3.0.zip
cd pdf-analyser
./scripts/install.sh
```

### Windows PowerShell, CPU mode

```powershell
Expand-Archive .\pdf-analyser-v0.3.0.zip
cd .\pdf-analyser
.\scripts\install.ps1
```

### Linux with an NVIDIA GPU

Docker must already be able to run:

```bash
docker run --rm --gpus all ubuntu nvidia-smi
```

Then:

```bash
./scripts/install-nvidia.sh
```

The first startup is slower because Docker builds the two application images and downloads Docling, GROBID, and the configured Ollama model.

## Manual installation

Copy the example configuration:

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

Edit `.env`, then start CPU mode:

```bash
docker compose -f compose.yaml up -d --build
```

Start NVIDIA mode:

```bash
docker compose \
  -f compose.yaml \
  -f compose.nvidia.yaml \
  up -d --build
```

Check the stack:

```bash
docker compose -f compose.yaml ps
```

Check runtime readiness:

```bash
curl -sS http://localhost:8090/health/ready | jq
curl -sS http://localhost:8100/health/ready | jq
curl -sS http://localhost:8100/v1/runtime | jq
```

Expected local endpoints:

| Service | URL |
|---|---|
| Tools API | `http://localhost:8090` |
| Tools Swagger | `http://localhost:8090/docs` |
| Agent API | `http://localhost:8100` |
| Agent Swagger | `http://localhost:8100/docs` |
| GROBID | `http://localhost:8086` |

Ollama is intentionally available only to the other containers and is not published on a host port.

## Choose the host output directory

Set `OUTPUT_DIR` in the root `.env` file:

```dotenv
OUTPUT_DIR=./outputs
```

Examples:

```dotenv
# Linux
OUTPUT_DIR=/srv/pdf-results

# macOS
OUTPUT_DIR=/Users/amir/pdf-results

# Windows Docker Desktop
OUTPUT_DIR=C:/Users/amir/pdf-results
```

The selected host path is bind-mounted into the Agent as `/data/outputs`.

## Choose a folder name for each PDF

The `output_folder` multipart field creates a named directory below `OUTPUT_DIR`.

```bash
curl \
  --fail \
  --show-error \
  -X POST \
  "http://localhost:8100/v1/process" \
  -F "file=@/absolute/path/VRX.pdf;type=application/pdf" \
  -F "use_llm=true" \
  -F "output_folder=VRX_case_001" \
  -F "overwrite=false" \
  -o agent-result.json
```

With:

```dotenv
OUTPUT_DIR=/srv/pdf-results
```

the result is written to:

```text
/srv/pdf-results/
├── VRX_case_001/
│   ├── canonical_document.json
│   ├── source/
│   ├── text/
│   ├── figures/
│   ├── tables/
│   ├── formulas/
│   └── latex/
└── VRX_case_001.zip
```

`output_folder` is a name, not a path. The API rejects path separators and traversal values such as `../outside`. Spaces are normalized to underscores. Names are limited to 100 characters, and Windows-reserved names such as `CON` and `LPT1` are rejected.

If the target already exists, the API returns HTTP `409` unless the request explicitly sends:

```bash
-F "overwrite=true"
```

When `output_folder` is omitted, the SHA-256 document ID remains the default folder name for backward compatibility.

## Output response

The response includes both the document identity and the chosen package folder:

```json
{
  "document_id": "f8a9...",
  "package_name": "VRX_case_001",
  "output_folder": "VRX_case_001",
  "package_directory": "/data/outputs/VRX_case_001",
  "canonical_json": "/data/outputs/VRX_case_001/canonical_document.json",
  "zip_file": "/data/outputs/VRX_case_001.zip"
}
```

The canonical JSON also records:

```json
{
  "output": {
    "folder_name": "VRX_case_001",
    "relative_path": "VRX_case_001",
    "zip_name": "VRX_case_001.zip"
  }
}
```

## Ollama model

The default model is:

```dotenv
OLLAMA_MODEL=qwen2.5vl:3b
```

The one-shot `ollama-model-init` service automatically pulls it into the persistent `ollama-models` Docker volume. Change `OLLAMA_MODEL` in `.env` and run:

```bash
make pull-model
```

or recreate the stack:

```bash
docker compose -f compose.yaml up -d
```

## Common commands

```bash
make init
make up-cpu
make up-gpu
make ready
make logs
make down
```

Process a PDF with a custom output folder:

```bash
make process \
  PDF=/absolute/path/VRX.pdf \
  USE_LLM=true \
  OUTPUT_FOLDER=VRX_case_001
```

Replace an existing package deliberately:

```bash
make process \
  PDF=/absolute/path/VRX.pdf \
  USE_LLM=true \
  OUTPUT_FOLDER=VRX_case_001 \
  OVERWRITE=true
```

## Portability

CPU mode is the portable deployment target for Docker Engine or Docker Desktop. The current release should be treated as an `linux/amd64` reference until the complete stack has also been integration-tested on ARM64 hardware. Windows and macOS users run the same Linux containers through Docker Desktop.

GPU mode is an optional acceleration layer:

- NVIDIA Linux/Windows hosts require a compatible NVIDIA driver and NVIDIA Container Toolkit;
- Docker Desktop GPU passthrough depends on the host platform;
- CPU mode remains available when GPU passthrough is unavailable, although extraction and vision inference are slower.

For a public binary-style release, publish the application images to GHCR or Docker Hub and replace the local `build:` sections with versioned `image:` references. The included source-build Compose file works without Python, Java, Docling, GROBID, or Ollama installed directly on the host; the host needs Docker Compose, internet access for the initial image/model downloads, and enough CPU/RAM/storage for the selected model.

## Security

The default `.env.example` binds APIs to `127.0.0.1` only. Do not change the bind host to `0.0.0.0` on an untrusted network without adding authentication, TLS, request-rate limits, and an upload policy.

## Development and validation

```bash
make test
make lint
make check
```

The package includes unit tests for:

- hierarchical sections and subsections;
- paragraph and visual-element section links;
- figure/table/formula LLM descriptions;
- safe custom output folder names;
- overwrite collision protection;
- GROBID liveness content negotiation.


===== FILE: SECURITY.md =====

# Security

This repository processes untrusted PDF uploads and should not be exposed directly to the public Internet without additional controls.

Before production deployment:

- place both APIs behind TLS and authentication;
- enforce upload size and request-rate limits at the reverse proxy;
- isolate containers and restrict outbound network access where possible;
- scan uploaded files according to your organization’s policy;
- keep GROBID admin and internal service ports private;
- use a non-root runtime and read-only container filesystem where practical;
- store secrets outside Git and rotate them regularly;
- apply retention policies to original PDFs and generated assets;
- review licenses and data-handling requirements for PDFs and models;
- monitor dependency and container-image vulnerabilities.

Report security issues privately to the repository owner rather than opening a public issue.

# Validation Report

Validation performed on the v0.3.0 packaged repository:

- Agent Ruff formatting: passed.
- Agent Ruff linting: passed.
- Agent unit tests: 24 passed.
- Agent Python compilation: passed.
- Nested TEI section/subsection hierarchy tests: passed.
- Section-to-paragraph and section-to-asset linking tests: passed.
- Vision-model description generation tests for figures, tables, and formulas: passed.
- Safe custom output-folder normalization and traversal protection tests: passed.
- Existing-package collision protection tests: passed.
- Tools Ruff formatting: passed.
- Tools Ruff linting: passed.
- Tools unit tests: 10 passed.
- GROBID `/api/isalive` and XML content-negotiation tests: passed.
- Tools Python compilation: passed.
- Root CPU Compose YAML parsed successfully.
- NVIDIA Compose override YAML parsed successfully.
- Standalone Tools and Agent Compose YAML parsed successfully.
- Linux installer shell syntax: passed.
- NVIDIA installer shell syntax: passed.
- Agent entrypoint shell syntax: passed.
- Public repository scan found no personal email address or user-specific local deployment path.

Not executed in the packaging environment:

- Docker Compose image builds;
- GROBID startup;
- Docling model downloads/inference;
- NVIDIA GPU inference;
- Ollama/Qwen inference;
- full HTTP end-to-end processing.

Validate the release on at least one clean CPU host and one NVIDIA host before publishing it as a production release.

===== FILE: agent/.env.example =====

# Agent API
APP_NAME=PDF Canonicalization Agent
APP_VERSION=0.3.0
LOG_LEVEL=INFO
AGENT_PORT=8100
MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB=100
MAX_CONCURRENCY=1

# Existing tools service. On Linux the Compose file maps host.docker.internal.
TOOLS_BASE_URL=http://host.docker.internal:8090
TOOLS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=1800
TOOLS_VERIFY_TLS=true

# Output packages
# This is the host parent directory. Each request may set output_folder.
OUTPUT_HOST_DIR=./data/outputs
HOST_UID=1000
HOST_GID=1000
KEEP_RAW_EXTRACTOR_RESPONSE=false
OVERWRITE_EXISTING_PACKAGE=false
CREATE_ZIP_PACKAGE=true

# Formula and fallback crops
FORMULA_CROP_SCALE=3.0
FORMULA_CROP_PADDING_POINTS=8.0
FALLBACK_ASSET_CROP_SCALE=2.5
FALLBACK_ASSET_CROP_PADDING_POINTS=5.0

# Optional bounded Llama/Ollama fallback. Deterministic parsing and quality checks
# work with this disabled.
LLM_ENABLED=false
OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://host.docker.internal:11434
OLLAMA_MODEL=qwen2.5vl:3b
OLLAMA_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=600
OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE=10m
OLLAMA_TEMPERATURE=0.0
LLM_VALIDATE_ALL_FIGURES=false
# When true, use Llama to generate a grounded description for every figure,
# table, and formula that has an image asset.
LLM_DESCRIBE_ALL_ELEMENTS=true
LLM_REPAIR_EMPTY_TABLES=true
LLM_REPAIR_UNCERTAIN_FORMULAS=true
LLM_MAX_ITEMS_PER_DOCUMENT=500

# Quality thresholds
QUALITY_GREEN_THRESHOLD=0.90
QUALITY_YELLOW_THRESHOLD=0.65

===== FILE: agent/CHANGELOG.md =====

# Changelog

## 0.3.0 - 2026-07-10

- Added a safe per-request `output_folder` field.
- Added optional per-request `overwrite` behavior with HTTP 409 collision protection.
- Added named-package static and ZIP download URLs.
- Added output-folder metadata to API responses and canonical JSON.
- Bumped the canonical JSON schema to `1.2.0`.
- Added Linux UID/GID mapping for host bind-mounted output directories.
- Added a root one-command Compose deployment with GROBID, Docling, Agent, Ollama, and automatic model pull.
- Added CPU and NVIDIA deployment modes.
- Added Linux/macOS and Windows installation scripts.
- Fixed GROBID health checks to use `/api/isalive` with endpoint-specific content negotiation.

## 0.2.0

- Preserve nested GROBID TEI sections and subsections.
- Add canonical section IDs, levels, parent IDs, full paths, child IDs, page ranges, and linked paragraph/asset IDs.
- Add canonical paragraph records and section links on Docling text blocks.
- Link figures, tables, and formulas to sections using exact GROBID TEI IDs when available, with page-range fallback and assignment confidence.
- Add optional Llama descriptions for every available figure, table, and formula image.
- Write `.description.txt` and `.description.json` files beside each described asset.
- Extend table repair and formula transcription responses with grounded descriptions.
- Add hierarchy, association, and all-element Llama description tests.

===== FILE: agent/Dockerfile =====

FROM python:3.12-slim-bookworm

ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
    PIP_NO_CACHE_DIR=1 \
    PIP_DISABLE_PIP_VERSION_CHECK=1

WORKDIR /app

RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
       ca-certificates \
       curl \
       gosu \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --upgrade pip \
    && pip install -r requirements.txt

COPY app ./app
COPY docker-entrypoint.sh /usr/local/bin/docker-entrypoint.sh

RUN useradd --create-home --uid 10002 appuser \
    && mkdir -p /data/outputs \
    && chown -R appuser:appuser /app /data/outputs \
    && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/docker-entrypoint.sh

EXPOSE 8100
ENTRYPOINT ["docker-entrypoint.sh"]
CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8100", "--workers", "1"]

.PHONY: init up down build logs ps test lint format check process

	@test -f .env || cp .env.example .env
	@mkdir -p data/outputs

up: init
	docker compose up -d --build

build:
	docker compose build

down:
	docker compose down

logs:
	docker compose logs -f agent-api

ps:
	docker compose ps

test:
	python -m pytest -q

lint:
	ruff check app tests

format:
	ruff format app tests
	ruff check --fix app tests

check:
	ruff format --check app tests
	ruff check app tests
	python -m pytest -q
	python -m compileall -q app

process:
	@test -n "$(PDF)" || (echo "Usage: make process PDF=/path/to/article.pdf [USE_LLM=true]" && exit 2)
	curl --fail --show-error -X POST http://localhost:$${AGENT_PORT:-8100}/v1/process \
	  -F "file=@$(PDF);type=application/pdf" \
	  -F "use_llm=$(if $(USE_LLM),$(USE_LLM),false)" | jq


===== FILE: agent/README.md =====

# PDF Canonicalization Agent

This service is the second stage of the `pdf-analyser` pipeline. It does **not** parse PDFs directly as its primary path. Instead, it:

1. receives a PDF;
2. sends the PDF to the existing `tools` extraction API;
3. receives Docling JSON/Markdown, GROBID TEI-derived JSON, and exported figure/table assets;
4. deterministically aligns and merges the two parser views;
5. runs quality checks per document and per element;
6. optionally uses a bounded local Llama vision model through Ollama to repair difficult tables/formulas and generate grounded descriptions for every available figure, table, and formula image;
7. writes one canonical JSON file plus all derived assets into a self-contained output folder;
8. optionally creates a ZIP archive of that folder.

The deterministic pipeline works without an LLM. Llama is an optional fallback, not the source of truth.

## Architecture

```text
PDF
 │
 ▼
Agent API :8100
 │
 ├── POST PDF to Tools API :8090
 │      ├── Docling
 │      │    ├── text/layout
 │      │    ├── structured tables
 │      │    ├── formulas
 │      │    └── figure/table images
 │      └── GROBID
 │           ├── metadata
 │           ├── sections
 │           ├── references
 │           ├── equation numbers
 │           └── figure/table labels and captions
 │
 ├── deterministic alignment and merge
 ├── quality gate
 ├── optional Ollama/Llama vision fallback
 ├── formula crops from the original PDF
 └── canonical package
```

## Output package

Each document is identified by its SHA-256 hash:

```text
data/outputs/
├── <sha256>/
│   ├── canonical_document.json       # the single canonical JSON file
│   ├── source/
│   │   └── original.pdf
│   ├── text/
│   │   ├── full_text.txt
│   │   └── full_text.md
│   ├── figures/
│   │   ├── figure_001.png
│   │   ├── figure_001.caption.txt
│   │   ├── figure_001.description.txt      # when Llama is enabled
│   │   ├── figure_001.description.json     # grounded structured description
│   │   └── ...
│   ├── tables/
│   │   ├── table_001.png
│   │   ├── table_001.caption.txt
│   │   ├── table_001.csv
│   │   ├── table_001.md
│   │   ├── table_001.html
│   │   ├── table_001.tex
│   │   ├── table_001.description.txt       # when Llama is enabled
│   │   ├── table_001.description.json
│   │   └── ...
│   ├── formulas/
│   │   ├── formula_001.png
│   │   ├── formula_001.txt
│   │   ├── formula_001.tex
│   │   ├── formula_001.description.txt     # when Llama is enabled
│   │   ├── formula_001.description.json
│   │   └── ...
│   └── latex/
│       ├── formulas.tex
│       ├── tables.tex
│       └── document_assets.tex
└── <sha256>.zip
```

`canonical_document.json` contains:

- document identity and source PDF path;
- title, authors, abstract, DOI, journal, date, and language from GROBID when available;
- Docling text blocks with page/bounding-box provenance and section links;
- a canonical article hierarchy with sections, subsections, parent IDs, levels, paths, child IDs, and section-linked paragraphs;
- a nested `section_tree` representation in addition to the flat section list;
- references from GROBID;
- figures with local image paths, captions, labels, section paths, alignment confidence, optional Llama descriptions, and both parser sources;
- tables with local images, columns, rows, inline LaTeX, CSV/Markdown/HTML/LaTeX paths, section paths, quality status, optional Llama repair/description evidence, and both parser sources;
- formulas with local image crops, LaTeX, plain text, equation numbers, section paths, parser candidates, optional Llama transcription/description evidence, and both parser sources;
- a document quality report;
- parser versions, counts, status, and LLM provenance.

## Canonical section hierarchy

The canonical JSON schema is `1.2.0`. A scientific article is represented both as a flat relational list and as a recursive tree:

```json
{
  "content": {
    "sections": [
      {
        "id": "sec_001",
        "title": "Methods",
        "number": "2",
        "level": 1,
        "parent_id": null,
        "path": ["Methods"],
        "child_section_ids": ["sec_002"],
        "paragraph_ids": ["para_00001"],
        "figure_ids": [],
        "table_ids": [],
        "formula_ids": []
      },
      {
        "id": "sec_002",
        "title": "Dataset",
        "number": "2.1",
        "level": 2,
        "parent_id": "sec_001",
        "path": ["Methods", "Dataset"],
        "child_section_ids": [],
        "paragraph_ids": ["para_00002"],
        "figure_ids": ["figure_001"],
        "table_ids": ["table_001"],
        "formula_ids": ["formula_001"]
      }
    ],
    "section_tree": [
      {
        "id": "sec_001",
        "title": "Methods",
        "children": [
          {
            "id": "sec_002",
            "title": "Dataset",
            "children": []
          }
        ]
      }
    ],
    "paragraphs": [
      {
        "id": "para_00002",
        "section_id": "sec_002",
        "section_path": ["Methods", "Dataset"],
        "text": "The dataset contains ..."
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Figures, tables, formulas, and matched Docling text blocks also receive `section_id`, `section_title`, and `section_path`.

## Prerequisites

### Required

Install these before starting the agent:

- Linux, macOS, or Windows with a current Docker installation;
- Docker Engine or Docker Desktop;
- Docker Compose v2 (`docker compose`, not the old `docker-compose` command);
- the `tools` service running and reachable on port `8090`;
- `curl` and `jq` for the command-line examples.

Recommended host resources for the full tools + agent stack:

- 8 CPU cores or more;
- 16 GB RAM minimum, 32 GB preferred for larger scientific PDFs;
- enough disk space for Docling/GROBID models and generated assets.

The agent itself does not require a GPU. GPU requirements belong to Docling in `../tools` and to the optional Ollama server.

### Optional: Ollama and Llama vision

The agent supports the Ollama REST API. On Linux, install Ollama:

```bash
curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh
```

Start or verify the service:

```bash
sudo systemctl enable --now ollama
curl http://localhost:11434/api/tags | jq
```

Pull the default model:

```bash
ollama pull llama3.2-vision:11b
```

Then set in `.env`:

```dotenv
LLM_ENABLED=true
OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://host.docker.internal:11434
OLLAMA_MODEL=llama3.2-vision:11b
```

Important for an 8 GB GTX 1080: Llama 3.2 Vision 11B is a large model. Docling GPU inference and Ollama may not fit in VRAM at the same time. Practical options are:

- keep the LLM disabled during normal extraction;
- run Ollama on another machine/GPU;
- run Docling on CPU and reserve the GPU for Ollama;
- use a remote Ollama-compatible endpoint;
- accept CPU offload and slower LLM repair.

The LLM is called only when requested with `use_llm=true`, when `LLM_ENABLED=true`, and while the per-document item budget has not been exceeded.

## Upgrade from Agent 0.1

The `make init` command does not overwrite an existing `.env`. If you already have `agent/.env`, add or update these values manually:

```dotenv
APP_VERSION=0.3.0
LLM_DESCRIBE_ALL_ELEMENTS=true
LLM_MAX_ITEMS_PER_DOCUMENT=500
```

Then rebuild the Agent container:

```bash
cd agent
docker compose up -d --build --force-recreate
```

The canonical JSON schema is now `1.2.0`. Existing packages remain readable; newly processed PDFs also include output-folder metadata while retaining `content.paragraphs`, `content.section_tree`, hierarchical section fields, section links on visual elements, and optional vision-model description assets.


Or from the repository root:

```bash

```

Verify:

```bash
curl -fsS http://localhost:8100/health/ready | jq
```

Expected:

```json
{
  "status": "ready",
  "tools_ready": true,
  "llm_enabled": false,
  "llm_alive": null,
  "ready": true
}
```

## Process a PDF

Without Llama:

```bash
curl --fail --show-error \
  -X POST http://localhost:8100/v1/process \
  -F "file=@/absolute/path/article.pdf;type=application/pdf" \
  -F "use_llm=false" \
  -o agent-result.json

jq . agent-result.json
```

With the configured Llama/Ollama fallback:

```bash
curl --fail --show-error \
  -X POST http://localhost:8100/v1/process \
  -F "file=@/absolute/path/article.pdf;type=application/pdf" \
  -F "use_llm=true" \
  -o agent-result.json
```

The response includes:

- document ID;
- output folder path;
- canonical JSON path;
- ZIP path;
- package URLs;
- quality status and score;
- element counts;
- warnings;
- extraction and canonicalization timings.

Example:

```json
{
  "status": "completed_with_warnings",
  "document_id": "...sha256...",
  "package_directory": "/data/outputs/...sha256...",
  "canonical_json": "/data/outputs/.../canonical_document.json",
  "zip_file": "/data/outputs/...sha256....zip",
  "quality": {
    "status": "yellow",
    "score": 0.85
  }
}
```

## Download and inspect results

Get the canonical JSON:

```bash
curl -o canonical_document.json \
  http://localhost:8100/v1/documents/<sha256>
```

Download the complete ZIP:

```bash
curl -OJ \
  http://localhost:8100/v1/documents/<sha256>/download
```

Direct static package paths are available under:

```text
http://localhost:8100/packages/<sha256>/
```

## Run from Python without Docker

The tools service still needs to be running.

```bash
cd agent
python3.12 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
cp -n .env.example .env
```

Set the tools URL for a host process:

```bash
export TOOLS_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8090
export OUTPUT_DIR="$PWD/data/outputs"
```

Run the CLI:

```bash
python -m app.cli /path/to/article.pdf
```

With Llama:

```bash
export LLM_ENABLED=true
export OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://localhost:11434
python -m app.cli /path/to/article.pdf --use-llm
```

## Merge strategy

The first version intentionally uses a conservative merge:

### Metadata

- GROBID is primary for title, authors, abstract, DOI, journal, publication date, and language.
- Docling identity is retained as source metadata.

### Text and article hierarchy

- Docling is primary for ordered text blocks and page/bounding-box provenance.
- The agent parses the raw GROBID TEI and preserves nested `<div>` structure rather than flattening it.
- Every canonical section includes `level`, `parent_id`, `path`, `child_section_ids`, `paragraph_ids`, page range, and linked figure/table/formula IDs.
- Every canonical paragraph includes `section_id` and `section_path`.
- Docling text blocks are matched to GROBID paragraphs/headings and receive the same section links when possible.
- A nested `section_tree` is generated for consumers that prefer recursive traversal.
- If raw TEI is unavailable, the agent falls back to the flat GROBID section list and infers subsection levels from section numbers such as `2.1` and `2.1.3`.
- The full Docling Markdown is exported as `text/full_text.md`.

### Figures

- Docling supplies image assets and PDF provenance.
- GROBID supplies figure labels/captions when available.
- Elements are matched using caption similarity, page agreement, and order.
- Each figure is linked to the nearest/exact GROBID section and receives `section_id`, `section_title`, and `section_path`.
- When Llama is enabled and `LLM_DESCRIBE_ALL_ELEMENTS=true`, every available figure image receives a grounded structured description.
- Llama descriptions are stored as derived data and never replace the original caption.

### Tables

- Docling TableFormer is primary for cells, rows, and columns.
- GROBID supplies table labels/captions and coordinates.
- Every table is linked to its article section when a reliable link can be established.
- Empty Docling table structures are quality failures.
- If Llama is enabled, empty tables can be transcribed into strict columns/rows JSON.
- With `LLM_DESCRIBE_ALL_ELEMENTS=true`, every available table image also receives a grounded description and summary. The repair call itself supplies the description when a repair is required.
- Llama output is marked as derived evidence; it never erases parser evidence.

### Formulas

- Docling is primary for enriched formula text/LaTeX and PDF provenance.
- GROBID supplies equation numbering and another text candidate.
- Every formula is linked to its article section when possible.
- The agent crops a high-resolution formula image directly from the original PDF using Docling coordinates.
- Optional Llama transcription is used when the parser result is empty or clearly malformed.
- With `LLM_DESCRIBE_ALL_ELEMENTS=true`, every available formula image receives a grounded structural description even when the parser LaTeX is already accepted.

## Quality gate

The quality report is embedded in `canonical_document.json`:

```json
{
  "quality": {
    "status": "green",
    "score": 0.95,
    "issues": []
  }
}
```

Current checks include:

- Docling/GROBID completion;
- required metadata presence;
- text presence;
- missing or invalid section hierarchy;
- unresolved element-to-section links;
- empty table structures;
- missing formula LaTeX;
- missing formula/figure image assets;
- parser count disagreements.

Status thresholds:

```dotenv
QUALITY_GREEN_THRESHOLD=0.90
QUALITY_YELLOW_THRESHOLD=0.65
```

These rules are deliberately transparent. Before production indexing, calibrate them against a manually reviewed set of your own PDFs.

## LLM safety and reproducibility

The LLM is bounded by these rules:

- disabled by default;
- explicitly requested per document;
- maximum model calls per document;
- optional descriptions for every figure, table, and formula image;
- temperature defaults to zero;
- Ollama structured JSON schemas are used;
- parser output is always retained;
- LLM output is labeled as derived evidence;
- LLM table values are not accepted silently—the quality record identifies repairs and warnings;
- no unrestricted autonomous loop is used.

## Configuration reference

Important variables:

| Variable | Default | Purpose |
|---|---:|---|
| `TOOLS_BASE_URL` | `http://host.docker.internal:8090` | Existing extraction API |
| `TOOLS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` | `1800` | Maximum tools request time |
| `OUTPUT_HOST_DIR` | `./data/outputs` | Host output directory |
| `FORMULA_CROP_SCALE` | `3.0` | Formula crop rendering scale |
| `LLM_ENABLED` | `false` | Enables Ollama calls in the service |
| `OLLAMA_BASE_URL` | `http://host.docker.internal:11434` | Ollama endpoint |
| `OLLAMA_MODEL` | `llama3.2-vision:11b` | Vision model |
| `LLM_DESCRIBE_ALL_ELEMENTS` | `true` | Describe every available figure, table, and formula image when Llama is used |
| `LLM_MAX_ITEMS_PER_DOCUMENT` | `500` | Hard LLM call budget; reduce this to cap cost/latency |
| `KEEP_RAW_EXTRACTOR_RESPONSE` | `false` | Save raw tools response for debugging |
| `CREATE_ZIP_PACKAGE` | `true` | Create downloadable ZIP |

See `.env.example` for the complete list.

## Tests

Local:

```bash
python3.12 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
make check
```

The tests cover:

- nested GROBID TEI section/subsection hierarchy;
- flat-section fallback and inferred subsection levels;
- paragraph, text-block, figure, table, and formula section links;
- Llama descriptions and description asset files for all visual element types;
- Docling reference resolution;
- table matrix reconstruction;
- Markdown/LaTeX generation;
- parser element matching;
- coordinate conversion and formula cropping;
- quality rules;
- settings defaults.

## Production notes

Before production deployment:

1. Put the tools and agent APIs behind a reverse proxy with TLS.
2. Add authentication and request authorization; these services currently trust callers on the network.
3. Use object storage for long-term assets instead of a local bind mount.
4. Add retention/cleanup rules for output packages.
5. Run one agent worker per writable output volume, or move package generation to a shared object store with distributed locking.
6. Keep `MAX_CONCURRENCY=1` initially because one request can trigger expensive PDF processing and optional vision inference.
7. Add metrics for parser duration, LLM calls, quality status, failures, and output size.
8. Scan uploaded PDFs and enforce upload limits.
9. Do not expose GROBID admin ports publicly.
10. Keep raw parser outputs during evaluation, even if disabled later in production.

For OpenSearch RAG, index from `canonical_document.json`, not directly from Docling or GROBID. Create separate chunk records for narrative text, tables, formulas, and figures while retaining `document_id`, page, bounding box, element ID, and asset path.

## Troubleshooting

### Agent is not ready

```bash
docker compose logs -f agent-api
curl http://localhost:8090/health/ready | jq
```

The agent is considered ready when the tools service is ready. The LLM is optional.

### Agent container cannot reach tools on Linux

The Compose file includes:

```yaml
extra_hosts:
  - "host.docker.internal:host-gateway"
```

Confirm tools binds to `0.0.0.0:8090`, not only `127.0.0.1`.

### Figure/table download fails

Confirm tools serves assets:

```bash
curl -I http://localhost:8090/assets/<sha256>/figures/figure_001.png
```

The agent falls back to a PDF provenance crop when an asset cannot be downloaded.

### Formula image is cropped incorrectly

Inspect the formula provenance in `canonical_document.json`. The cropper converts Docling `BOTTOMLEFT` coordinates to PyMuPDF page coordinates. Increase:

```dotenv
FORMULA_CROP_PADDING_POINTS=12
FORMULA_CROP_SCALE=4.0
```

### Empty table structure

The canonical table will show:

```json
{
  "data_source": "none",
  "quality": {
    "status": "failed"
  }
}
```

Enable Ollama only after reviewing the exported table image. Llama repair is a fallback, not guaranteed ground truth.

### Ollama unavailable

```bash
curl http://localhost:11434/api/tags | jq
ollama list
ollama pull llama3.2-vision:11b
```

The document still completes without LLM enrichment and records a warning.

## Custom output root and package folder names

The standalone Agent Compose file uses `OUTPUT_HOST_DIR` for the host parent directory. The root all-in-one Compose stack uses `OUTPUT_DIR`.

Each `/v1/process` request can also provide a safe package folder name:

```bash
curl \
  --fail \
  --show-error \
  -X POST \
  http://localhost:8100/v1/process \
  -F "file=@/absolute/path/article.pdf;type=application/pdf" \
  -F "use_llm=true" \
  -F "output_folder=article_run_001" \
  -F "overwrite=false" \
  -o agent-result.json
```

The Agent writes the package to:

```text
<configured-output-root>/article_run_001/
```

and writes the ZIP to:

```text
<configured-output-root>/article_run_001.zip
```

The output folder is strictly a single name. Path separators and traversal values are rejected. If a package already exists, the request returns HTTP 409 unless `overwrite=true` is supplied.


===== FILE: agent/app/__init__.py =====



===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/__init__.py =====



===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/builder.py =====

import shutil
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.canonical.coordinates import region_from_docling, regions_from_grobid
from app.canonical.cropper import crop_grobid_region, crop_region
from app.canonical.exporters import (
    latex_escape,
    normalize_llm_table,
    table_matrix,
    table_to_latex,
    write_table_assets,
)
from app.canonical.matching import best_match
from app.canonical.quality import build_quality_report
from app.canonical.refs import referenced_texts
from app.canonical.sections import (
    assign_text_blocks_to_sections,
    attach_elements_to_sections,
    build_section_structure,
    build_section_tree,
)
from app.clients.extractor import ExtractorClient
from app.clients.ollama import OllamaClient
from app.config import Settings
from app.utils import (
    atomic_write_json,
    atomic_write_text,
    recreate_directory,
    relative_posix,
    safe_output_folder,
)


class CanonicalPackageBuilder:
    def __init__(
        self,
        settings: Settings,
        *,
        extractor: ExtractorClient,
        llm: OllamaClient,
    ) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self.extractor = extractor
        self.llm = llm

    async def build(
        self,
        *,
        pdf_path: Path,
        filename: str,
        extraction: dict[str, Any],
        use_llm: bool,
        output_folder: str | None = None,
        overwrite: bool | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        document = extraction.get("document") or {}
        document_id = str(document.get("sha256") or "unknown")
        package_name = safe_output_folder(output_folder) if output_folder else document_id
        package_dir = self.settings.output_dir / package_name
        zip_candidate = self.settings.output_dir / f"{package_name}.zip"
        should_overwrite = (
            self.settings.overwrite_existing_package if overwrite is None else overwrite
        )

        if not should_overwrite and (package_dir.exists() or zip_candidate.exists()):
            raise FileExistsError(
                f"Output folder '{package_name}' already exists. "
                "Choose another output_folder or submit overwrite=true."
            )

        if should_overwrite:
            recreate_directory(package_dir)
            zip_candidate.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        else:
            package_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)

        source_dir = package_dir / "source"
        text_dir = package_dir / "text"
        figures_dir = package_dir / "figures"
        tables_dir = package_dir / "tables"
        formulas_dir = package_dir / "formulas"
        latex_dir = package_dir / "latex"
        for directory in (
            source_dir,
            text_dir,
            figures_dir,
            tables_dir,
            formulas_dir,
            latex_dir,
        ):
            directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        source_pdf = source_dir / "original.pdf"
        shutil.copy2(pdf_path, source_pdf)

        if self.settings.keep_raw_extractor_response:
            atomic_write_json(source_dir / "extractor-response.json", extraction)

        docling = extraction.get("docling") or {}
        grobid = extraction.get("grobid") or {}
        docling_raw = docling.get("raw") or {}
        docling_document = docling_raw.get("document_json") or {}
        docling_parsed = docling.get("parsed") or {}
        grobid_parsed = grobid.get("parsed") or {}
        grobid_raw = grobid.get("raw") or {}

        warnings: list[str] = [str(value) for value in extraction.get("warnings") or []]
        llm_budget = self.settings.llm_max_items_per_document
        llm_used = 0
        llm_available = use_llm and self.settings.llm_enabled and await self.llm.is_alive()
        if use_llm and self.settings.llm_enabled and not llm_available:
            warnings.append(
                "LLM enrichment was requested but the configured Ollama service is unavailable."
            )

        metadata = self._metadata(grobid_parsed, docling_document, filename, document_id)
        text_content = self._text_content(docling_document, docling_raw, grobid_parsed)
        section_structure = build_section_structure(
            tei_xml=grobid_raw.get("tei_xml"),
            flat_sections=grobid_parsed.get("sections") or [],
        )
        assign_text_blocks_to_sections(
            text_content["text_blocks"],
            sections=section_structure["sections"],
            paragraphs=section_structure["paragraphs"],
        )
        atomic_write_text(text_dir / "full_text.txt", text_content["full_text"])
        atomic_write_text(text_dir / "full_text.md", text_content["markdown"])

        figures, figure_warnings, figure_llm_used = await self._build_figures(
            pdf_path=pdf_path,
            package_dir=package_dir,
            output_dir=figures_dir,
            docling_document=docling_document,
            docling_parsed=docling_parsed,
            grobid_parsed=grobid_parsed,
            llm_available=llm_available,
            llm_budget=max(0, llm_budget - llm_used),
        )
        warnings.extend(figure_warnings)
        llm_used += figure_llm_used

        tables, table_warnings, table_llm_used, combined_tables_latex = await self._build_tables(
            pdf_path=pdf_path,
            package_dir=package_dir,
            output_dir=tables_dir,
            docling_document=docling_document,
            docling_parsed=docling_parsed,
            grobid_parsed=grobid_parsed,
            llm_available=llm_available,
            llm_budget=max(0, llm_budget - llm_used),
        )
        warnings.extend(table_warnings)
        llm_used += table_llm_used

        (
            formulas,
            formula_warnings,
            formula_llm_used,
            combined_formulas_latex,
        ) = await self._build_formulas(
            pdf_path=pdf_path,
            package_dir=package_dir,
            output_dir=formulas_dir,
            docling_parsed=docling_parsed,
            grobid_parsed=grobid_parsed,
            llm_available=llm_available,
            llm_budget=max(0, llm_budget - llm_used),
        )
        warnings.extend(formula_warnings)
        llm_used += formula_llm_used

        attach_elements_to_sections(
            sections=section_structure["sections"],
            source_element_to_section=section_structure["source_element_to_section"],
            figures=figures,
            tables=tables,
            formulas=formulas,
        )
        section_tree = build_section_tree(section_structure["sections"])

        atomic_write_text(latex_dir / "tables.tex", combined_tables_latex)
        atomic_write_text(latex_dir / "formulas.tex", combined_formulas_latex)
        master_latex = self._master_latex(figures, tables, formulas)
        atomic_write_text(latex_dir / "document_assets.tex", master_latex)

        canonical: dict[str, Any] = {
            "schema_version": "1.2.0",
            "document_id": document_id,
            "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
            "output": {
                "folder_name": package_name,
                "relative_path": package_name,
                "zip_name": f"{package_name}.zip" if self.settings.create_zip_package else None,
            },
            "document": {
                "filename": filename,
                "sha256": document_id,
                "size_bytes": document.get("size_bytes"),
                "source_pdf": relative_posix(source_pdf, package_dir),
            },
            "metadata": metadata,
            "content": {
                "full_text": text_content["full_text"],
                "text_blocks": text_content["text_blocks"],
                "paragraphs": section_structure["paragraphs"],
                "sections": section_structure["sections"],
                "section_tree": section_tree,
                "figures": figures,
                "tables": tables,
                "formulas": formulas,
                "references": grobid_parsed.get("references") or [],
            },
            "assets": {
                "text": {
                    "plain_text": "text/full_text.txt",
                    "markdown": "text/full_text.md",
                },
                "latex": {
                    "formulas": "latex/formulas.tex",
                    "tables": "latex/tables.tex",
                    "master": "latex/document_assets.tex",
                },
            },
            "provenance": {
                "extractor_schema_version": extraction.get("schema_version"),
                "extractor_request_id": extraction.get("request_id"),
                "extractor_status": extraction.get("status"),
                "docling": {
                    "status": docling.get("status"),
                    "version": docling.get("version"),
                    "stats": docling.get("stats") or {},
                },
                "grobid": {
                    "status": grobid.get("status"),
                    "version": grobid.get("version"),
                    "stats": grobid.get("stats") or {},
                    "section_structure_source": section_structure.get("source"),
                },
                "llm": {
                    "enabled_in_service": self.settings.llm_enabled,
                    "requested_for_document": use_llm,
                    "available": llm_available,
                    "provider": self.settings.llm_provider if self.settings.llm_enabled else None,
                    "model": self.settings.ollama_model if self.settings.llm_enabled else None,
                    "items_processed": llm_used,
                    "describe_all_elements": self.settings.llm_describe_all_elements,
                    "max_items_per_document": self.settings.llm_max_items_per_document,
                    "role": (
                        "bounded visual repair and description for figures, tables, and formulas; "
                        "parser evidence is always retained"
                    ),
                },
            },
            "warnings": list(dict.fromkeys(warnings)),
        }
        canonical["quality"] = build_quality_report(
            canonical,
            extraction=extraction,
            settings=self.settings,
        )
        canonical["counts"] = {
            "text_blocks": len(text_content["text_blocks"]),
            "paragraphs": len(canonical["content"]["paragraphs"]),
            "sections": len(canonical["content"]["sections"]),
            "root_sections": len(canonical["content"]["section_tree"]),
            "figures": len(figures),
            "tables": len(tables),
            "formulas": len(formulas),
            "references": len(canonical["content"]["references"]),
        }

        canonical_path = package_dir / "canonical_document.json"
        atomic_write_json(canonical_path, canonical)

        zip_path: Path | None = None
        if self.settings.create_zip_package:
            archive = shutil.make_archive(
                str(self.settings.output_dir / package_name),
                "zip",
                root_dir=package_dir,
            )
            zip_path = Path(archive)

        return {
            "document_id": document_id,
            "package_name": package_name,
            "package_dir": package_dir,
            "canonical_path": canonical_path,
            "zip_path": zip_path,
            "canonical": canonical,
        }

    def _metadata(
        self,
        grobid_parsed: dict[str, Any],
        docling_document: dict[str, Any],
        filename: str,
        document_id: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        metadata = dict(grobid_parsed.get("metadata") or {})
        metadata.setdefault("title", None)
        metadata.setdefault("authors", [])
        metadata.setdefault("abstract", None)
        metadata.setdefault("doi", None)
        metadata.setdefault("journal", None)
        metadata.setdefault("publication_date", None)
        metadata.setdefault("language", None)
        if not metadata.get("title"):
            metadata["title"] = docling_document.get("name") or Path(filename).stem
        metadata["source_filename"] = filename
        metadata["document_id"] = document_id
        metadata["sources"] = {
            "primary": "grobid",
            "docling_document_name": docling_document.get("name"),
        }
        return metadata

    def _text_content(
        self,
        docling_document: dict[str, Any],
        docling_raw: dict[str, Any],
        grobid_parsed: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        excluded = {"formula", "page_header", "page_footer"}
        blocks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        full_text_parts: list[str] = []
        for index, item in enumerate(docling_document.get("texts") or [], start=1):
            label = str(item.get("label") or "text")
            if label.lower() in excluded:
                continue
            text = item.get("text") or item.get("orig")
            if not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip():
                continue
            clean = " ".join(text.split())
            block = {
                "id": item.get("self_ref") or f"text_{index:05d}",
                "index": index,
                "label": label,
                "text": clean,
                "original": item.get("orig"),
                "provenance": item.get("prov") or [],
            }
            blocks.append(block)
            full_text_parts.append(clean)

        if not blocks:
            fallback_index = 0
            for section in grobid_parsed.get("sections") or []:
                heading = section.get("heading")
                if heading:
                    fallback_index += 1
                    blocks.append(
                        {
                            "id": f"grobid_text_{fallback_index:05d}",
                            "index": fallback_index,
                            "label": "section_header",
                            "text": heading,
                            "original": heading,
                            "provenance": [],
                            "source": "grobid_fallback",
                        }
                    )
                    full_text_parts.append(heading)
                for paragraph in section.get("paragraphs") or []:
                    if not isinstance(paragraph, str) or not paragraph.strip():
                        continue
                    fallback_index += 1
                    clean = " ".join(paragraph.split())
                    blocks.append(
                        {
                            "id": f"grobid_text_{fallback_index:05d}",
                            "index": fallback_index,
                            "label": "paragraph",
                            "text": clean,
                            "original": paragraph,
                            "provenance": [],
                            "source": "grobid_fallback",
                        }
                    )
                    full_text_parts.append(clean)

        markdown = str(docling_raw.get("markdown") or "").strip()
        if not markdown:
            sections = grobid_parsed.get("sections") or []
            markdown = "\n\n".join(
                "\n\n".join(
                    filter(
                        None,
                        [
                            f"## {section.get('heading')}" if section.get("heading") else None,
                            "\n\n".join(section.get("paragraphs") or []),
                        ],
                    )
                )
                for section in sections
            )
        return {
            "text_blocks": blocks,
            "full_text": "\n\n".join(full_text_parts),
            "markdown": markdown + ("\n" if markdown else ""),
        }

    async def _materialize_asset(
        self,
        *,
        asset: dict[str, Any] | None,
        destination: Path,
        pdf_path: Path,
        provenance: list[dict[str, Any]] | None,
        crop_scale: float,
        crop_padding: float,
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, list[str]]:
        warnings: list[str] = []
        if asset and asset.get("status") == "saved" and asset.get("url"):
            try:
                await self.extractor.download_asset(str(asset["url"]), destination)
                return (
                    {
                        "path": destination.name,
                        "width": asset.get("width"),
                        "height": asset.get("height"),
                        "size_bytes": destination.stat().st_size,
                        "source": "docling_export",
                    },
                    warnings,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                warnings.append(f"Could not download extractor asset {asset.get('url')}: {exc}")

        crop = crop_region(
            pdf_path,
            provenance=provenance,
            destination=destination,
            scale=crop_scale,
            padding_points=crop_padding,
        )
        if crop.get("status") == "saved":
            return (
                {
                    "path": destination.name,
                    "width": crop.get("width"),
                    "height": crop.get("height"),
                    "size_bytes": crop.get("size_bytes"),
                    "source": "pdf_provenance_crop",
                    "page": crop.get("page"),
                },
                warnings,
            )
        warnings.append(str(crop.get("error") or "Asset could not be materialized."))
        return None, warnings

    def _description_record(
        self,
        payload: dict[str, Any] | None,
        *,
        element_type: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        if not payload:
            return None
        text = str(payload.get("description") or "").strip()
        summary = str(payload.get("summary") or "").strip()
        if not text and not summary:
            return None
        excluded = {
            "description",
            "summary",
            "confidence",
            "warnings",
            "columns",
            "rows",
            "latex",
            "plain_text",
        }
        details = {key: value for key, value in payload.items() if key not in excluded}
        return {
            "text": text or summary,
            "summary": summary or None,
            "element_type": element_type,
            "source": "ollama_vision",
            "provider": self.settings.llm_provider,
            "model": self.settings.ollama_model,
            "confidence": payload.get("confidence"),
            "warnings": payload.get("warnings") or [],
            "details": details,
        }

    def _write_description_assets(
        self,
        *,
        output_dir: Path,
        package_dir: Path,
        stem: str,
        description: dict[str, Any] | None,
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        if not description:
            return {}
        text_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.description.txt"
        json_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.description.json"
        parts = [
            value
            for value in (description.get("summary"), description.get("text"))
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip()
        ]
        atomic_write_text(text_path, "\n\n".join(dict.fromkeys(parts)) + "\n")
        atomic_write_json(json_path, description)
        return {
            "description_text": relative_posix(text_path, package_dir),
            "description_json": relative_posix(json_path, package_dir),
        }

    async def _build_figures(
        self,
        *,
        pdf_path: Path,
        package_dir: Path,
        output_dir: Path,
        docling_document: dict[str, Any],
        docling_parsed: dict[str, Any],
        grobid_parsed: dict[str, Any],
        llm_available: bool,
        llm_budget: int,
    ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[str], int]:
        warnings: list[str] = []
        llm_used = 0
        docling_figures = docling_parsed.get("pictures") or []
        grobid_figures = [
            item
            for item in grobid_parsed.get("figures_and_tables") or []
            if item.get("type") != "table"
        ]
        used_grobid: set[int] = set()
        output: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for index, item in enumerate(docling_figures, start=1):
            captions = item.get("captions") or []
            caption = captions[0] if captions else None
            match_index, match_score, match_parts = best_match(
                item,
                grobid_figures,
                source_text=lambda value: " ".join(value.get("captions") or []),
                candidate_text=lambda value: " ".join(
                    filter(None, [value.get("label"), value.get("caption")])
                ),
                used_indexes=used_grobid,
                source_index=index - 1,
            )
            grobid_item = grobid_figures[match_index] if match_index is not None else None
            if match_index is not None:
                used_grobid.add(match_index)
            if not caption and grobid_item:
                caption = grobid_item.get("caption")

            stem = f"figure_{index:03d}"
            image_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.png"
            image_asset, asset_warnings = await self._materialize_asset(
                asset=item.get("asset"),
                destination=image_path,
                pdf_path=pdf_path,
                provenance=item.get("provenance") or [],
                crop_scale=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_scale,
                crop_padding=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_padding_points,
            )
            warnings.extend(f"{stem}: {warning}" for warning in asset_warnings)
            if image_asset:
                image_asset["path"] = relative_posix(image_path, package_dir)

            description: dict[str, Any] | None = None
            should_describe = (
                llm_available
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
                and (
                    self.settings.llm_describe_all_elements
                    or self.settings.llm_validate_all_figures
                    or not caption
                )
            )
            if should_describe:
                try:
                    raw_description = await self.llm.describe_figure(
                        image_path,
                        caption=caption,
                    )
                    description = self._description_record(
                        raw_description,
                        element_type="figure",
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                except Exception as exc:
                    warnings.append(f"{stem}: LLM figure description failed: {exc}")

            description_assets = self._write_description_assets(
                output_dir=output_dir,
                package_dir=package_dir,
                stem=stem,
                description=description,
            )
            caption_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.caption.txt"
            atomic_write_text(caption_path, (caption or "") + ("\n" if caption else ""))
            region = region_from_docling(item.get("provenance") or [])
            output.append(
                {
                    "id": stem,
                    "index": index,
                    "label": grobid_item.get("label") if grobid_item else None,
                    "caption": caption,
                    "description": description,
                    "page": region.page if region else None,
                    "provenance": {
                        "docling": item.get("provenance") or [],
                        "grobid_coords": grobid_item.get("coords") if grobid_item else None,
                    },
                    "alignment": {
                        "matched": grobid_item is not None,
                        "confidence": round(match_score, 4),
                        "components": match_parts,
                        "grobid_id": grobid_item.get("id") if grobid_item else None,
                    },
                    "assets": {
                        "image": image_asset,
                        "caption_text": relative_posix(caption_path, package_dir),
                        **description_assets,
                    },
                    "sources": {
                        "docling_id": item.get("id"),
                        "grobid": grobid_item,
                    },
                }
            )

        for grobid_index, grobid_item in enumerate(grobid_figures):
            if grobid_index in used_grobid:
                continue
            index = len(output) + 1
            stem = f"figure_{index:03d}"
            image_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.png"
            crop = crop_grobid_region(
                pdf_path,
                coords=grobid_item.get("coords"),
                destination=image_path,
                scale=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_scale,
                padding_points=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_padding_points,
            )
            image_asset = None
            if crop.get("status") == "saved":
                image_asset = {
                    "path": relative_posix(image_path, package_dir),
                    "width": crop.get("width"),
                    "height": crop.get("height"),
                    "size_bytes": crop.get("size_bytes"),
                    "page": crop.get("page"),
                    "source": "grobid_provenance_crop",
                }
            else:
                warnings.append(f"{stem}: {crop.get('error')}")

            caption = grobid_item.get("caption")
            description = None
            if (
                llm_available
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
                and (
                    self.settings.llm_describe_all_elements
                    or self.settings.llm_validate_all_figures
                    or not caption
                )
            ):
                try:
                    raw_description = await self.llm.describe_figure(
                        image_path,
                        caption=caption,
                    )
                    description = self._description_record(
                        raw_description,
                        element_type="figure",
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                except Exception as exc:
                    warnings.append(f"{stem}: LLM figure description failed: {exc}")
            description_assets = self._write_description_assets(
                output_dir=output_dir,
                package_dir=package_dir,
                stem=stem,
                description=description,
            )
            caption_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.caption.txt"
            atomic_write_text(caption_path, (caption or "") + ("\n" if caption else ""))
            grobid_regions = regions_from_grobid(grobid_item.get("coords"))
            output.append(
                {
                    "id": stem,
                    "index": index,
                    "label": grobid_item.get("label"),
                    "caption": caption,
                    "description": description,
                    "page": grobid_regions[0].page if grobid_regions else None,
                    "provenance": {"docling": [], "grobid_coords": grobid_item.get("coords")},
                    "alignment": {
                        "matched": False,
                        "confidence": 0.0,
                        "components": {},
                        "grobid_id": grobid_item.get("id"),
                        "status": "grobid_only",
                    },
                    "assets": {
                        "image": image_asset,
                        "caption_text": relative_posix(caption_path, package_dir),
                        **description_assets,
                    },
                    "sources": {"docling_id": None, "grobid": grobid_item},
                }
            )
        return output, warnings, llm_used

    async def _build_tables(
        self,
        *,
        pdf_path: Path,
        package_dir: Path,
        output_dir: Path,
        docling_document: dict[str, Any],
        docling_parsed: dict[str, Any],
        grobid_parsed: dict[str, Any],
        llm_available: bool,
        llm_budget: int,
    ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[str], int, str]:
        warnings: list[str] = []
        llm_used = 0
        raw_tables = docling_document.get("tables") or []
        parsed_tables = docling_parsed.get("tables") or []
        grobid_tables = [
            item
            for item in grobid_parsed.get("figures_and_tables") or []
            if item.get("type") == "table"
        ]
        used_grobid: set[int] = set()
        output: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        combined_latex: list[str] = []

        for index, raw_item in enumerate(raw_tables, start=1):
            parsed = parsed_tables[index - 1] if index <= len(parsed_tables) else {}
            captions = parsed.get("captions") or referenced_texts(
                docling_document,
                raw_item.get("captions") or [],
            )
            caption = captions[0] if captions else None
            caption_source = " ".join(captions)
            match_index, match_score, match_parts = best_match(
                parsed or raw_item,
                grobid_tables,
                source_text=lambda value, fallback=caption_source: " ".join(
                    value.get("captions") or [fallback]
                ),
                candidate_text=lambda value: " ".join(
                    filter(None, [value.get("label"), value.get("caption")])
                ),
                used_indexes=used_grobid,
                source_index=index - 1,
            )
            grobid_item = grobid_tables[match_index] if match_index is not None else None
            if match_index is not None:
                used_grobid.add(match_index)
            if not caption and grobid_item:
                caption = grobid_item.get("caption")

            stem = f"table_{index:03d}"
            image_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.png"
            image_asset, asset_warnings = await self._materialize_asset(
                asset=parsed.get("asset"),
                destination=image_path,
                pdf_path=pdf_path,
                provenance=parsed.get("provenance") or raw_item.get("prov") or [],
                crop_scale=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_scale,
                crop_padding=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_padding_points,
            )
            warnings.extend(f"{stem}: {warning}" for warning in asset_warnings)
            if image_asset:
                image_asset["path"] = relative_posix(image_path, package_dir)

            columns, rows, structure_warnings = table_matrix(raw_item)
            data_source = "docling_tableformer" if columns else "none"
            repair: dict[str, Any] | None = None
            if (
                not columns
                and llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_repair_empty_tables
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    repair = await self.llm.repair_table(image_path, caption=caption)
                    llm_used += 1
                    repaired_columns, repaired_rows, repair_warnings = normalize_llm_table(repair)
                    if repaired_columns and repaired_rows:
                        columns, rows = repaired_columns, repaired_rows
                        data_source = "ollama_vision_repair"
                        structure_warnings.extend(repair_warnings)
                    else:
                        structure_warnings.append(
                            "The vision model returned no usable table rows or columns."
                        )
                except Exception as exc:
                    structure_warnings.append(f"LLM table repair failed: {exc}")

            description = self._description_record(
                repair,
                element_type="table",
            )
            if (
                description is None
                and llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_describe_all_elements
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    raw_description = await self.llm.describe_table(
                        image_path,
                        caption=caption,
                        columns=columns,
                        rows=rows,
                    )
                    description = self._description_record(
                        raw_description,
                        element_type="table",
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                except Exception as exc:
                    structure_warnings.append(f"LLM table description failed: {exc}")

            description_assets = self._write_description_assets(
                output_dir=output_dir,
                package_dir=package_dir,
                stem=stem,
                description=description,
            )
            asset_names = write_table_assets(
                output_dir,
                stem=stem,
                columns=columns,
                rows=rows,
                caption=caption,
                latex_label=f"tab:{stem}",
            )
            latex_text = table_to_latex(
                columns,
                rows,
                caption=caption,
                label=f"tab:{stem}",
            )
            combined_latex.append(latex_text)
            caption_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.caption.txt"
            atomic_write_text(caption_path, (caption or "") + ("\n" if caption else ""))
            region = region_from_docling(parsed.get("provenance") or raw_item.get("prov") or [])
            if columns and rows:
                quality_status = "repaired" if data_source == "ollama_vision_repair" else "accepted"
            else:
                quality_status = "failed"

            output.append(
                {
                    "id": stem,
                    "index": index,
                    "label": grobid_item.get("label") if grobid_item else None,
                    "caption": caption,
                    "description": description,
                    "page": region.page if region else None,
                    "columns": columns,
                    "rows": rows,
                    "row_count": len(rows),
                    "column_count": len(columns),
                    "data_source": data_source,
                    "latex": latex_text,
                    "quality": {
