                        "status": quality_status,
                        "warnings": list(dict.fromkeys(structure_warnings)),
                        "llm_repair": repair,
                    },
                    "provenance": {
                        "docling": parsed.get("provenance") or raw_item.get("prov") or [],
                        "grobid_coords": grobid_item.get("coords") if grobid_item else None,
                    },
                    "alignment": {
                        "matched": grobid_item is not None,
                        "confidence": round(match_score, 4),
                        "components": match_parts,
                        "grobid_id": grobid_item.get("id") if grobid_item else None,
                    },
                    "assets": {
                        "image": image_asset,
                        "caption_text": relative_posix(caption_path, package_dir),
                        **description_assets,
                        **{
                            key: relative_posix(output_dir / filename, package_dir)
                            for key, filename in asset_names.items()
                        },
                    },
                    "sources": {
                        "docling_id": raw_item.get("self_ref"),
                        "docling_structure_status": parsed.get("structure_status"),
                        "grobid": grobid_item,
                    },
                }
            )

        for grobid_index, grobid_item in enumerate(grobid_tables):
            if grobid_index in used_grobid:
                continue
            index = len(output) + 1
            stem = f"table_{index:03d}"
            image_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.png"
            crop = crop_grobid_region(
                pdf_path,
                coords=grobid_item.get("coords"),
                destination=image_path,
                scale=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_scale,
                padding_points=self.settings.fallback_asset_crop_padding_points,
            )
            image_asset = None
            if crop.get("status") == "saved":
                image_asset = {
                    "path": relative_posix(image_path, package_dir),
                    "width": crop.get("width"),
                    "height": crop.get("height"),
                    "size_bytes": crop.get("size_bytes"),
                    "page": crop.get("page"),
                    "source": "grobid_provenance_crop",
                }
            else:
                warnings.append(f"{stem}: {crop.get('error')}")

            caption = grobid_item.get("caption")
            columns: list[str] = []
            rows: list[list[str]] = []
            structure_warnings = [
                "This table was detected only by GROBID and has no Docling cell structure."
            ]
            repair = None
            data_source = "grobid_flat_text"
            if (
                llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_repair_empty_tables
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    repair = await self.llm.repair_table(image_path, caption=caption)
                    llm_used += 1
                    columns, rows, repair_warnings = normalize_llm_table(repair)
                    structure_warnings.extend(repair_warnings)
                    if columns and rows:
                        data_source = "ollama_vision_repair"
                except Exception as exc:
                    structure_warnings.append(f"LLM table repair failed: {exc}")

            description = self._description_record(
                repair,
                element_type="table",
            )
            if (
                description is None
                and llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_describe_all_elements
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    raw_description = await self.llm.describe_table(
                        image_path,
                        caption=caption,
                        columns=columns,
                        rows=rows,
                    )
                    description = self._description_record(
                        raw_description,
                        element_type="table",
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                except Exception as exc:
                    structure_warnings.append(f"LLM table description failed: {exc}")

            description_assets = self._write_description_assets(
                output_dir=output_dir,
                package_dir=package_dir,
                stem=stem,
                description=description,
            )
            asset_names = write_table_assets(
                output_dir,
                stem=stem,
                columns=columns,
                rows=rows,
                caption=caption,
                latex_label=f"tab:{stem}",
            )
            latex_text = table_to_latex(columns, rows, caption=caption, label=f"tab:{stem}")
            combined_latex.append(latex_text)
            caption_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.caption.txt"
            atomic_write_text(caption_path, (caption or "") + ("\n" if caption else ""))
            grobid_regions = regions_from_grobid(grobid_item.get("coords"))
            quality_status = "repaired" if columns and rows else "failed"
            output.append(
                {
                    "id": stem,
                    "index": index,
                    "label": grobid_item.get("label"),
                    "caption": caption,
                    "description": description,
                    "page": grobid_regions[0].page if grobid_regions else None,
                    "columns": columns,
                    "rows": rows,
                    "row_count": len(rows),
                    "column_count": len(columns),
                    "data_source": data_source,
                    "flattened_text": grobid_item.get("content"),
                    "latex": latex_text,
                    "quality": {
                        "status": quality_status,
                        "warnings": list(dict.fromkeys(structure_warnings)),
                        "llm_repair": repair,
                    },
                    "provenance": {"docling": [], "grobid_coords": grobid_item.get("coords")},
                    "alignment": {
                        "matched": False,
                        "confidence": 0.0,
                        "components": {},
                        "grobid_id": grobid_item.get("id"),
                        "status": "grobid_only",
                    },
                    "assets": {
                        "image": image_asset,
                        "caption_text": relative_posix(caption_path, package_dir),
                        **description_assets,
                        **{
                            key: relative_posix(output_dir / filename, package_dir)
                            for key, filename in asset_names.items()
                        },
                    },
                    "sources": {
                        "docling_id": None,
                        "docling_structure_status": "missing",
                        "grobid": grobid_item,
                    },
                }
            )
        return output, warnings, llm_used, "\n".join(combined_latex)

    async def _build_formulas(
        self,
        *,
        pdf_path: Path,
        package_dir: Path,
        output_dir: Path,
        docling_parsed: dict[str, Any],
        grobid_parsed: dict[str, Any],
        llm_available: bool,
        llm_budget: int,
    ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[str], int, str]:
        warnings: list[str] = []
        llm_used = 0
        docling_formulas = docling_parsed.get("formulas") or []
        grobid_formulas = grobid_parsed.get("formulas") or []
        used_grobid: set[int] = set()
        output: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        combined_latex: list[str] = []

        for index, item in enumerate(docling_formulas, start=1):
            match_index, match_score, match_parts = best_match(
                item,
                grobid_formulas,
                source_text=lambda value: value.get("text") or value.get("original"),
                candidate_text=lambda value: value.get("text"),
                used_indexes=used_grobid,
                source_index=index - 1,
            )
            grobid_item = grobid_formulas[match_index] if match_index is not None else None
            if match_index is not None:
                used_grobid.add(match_index)

            stem = f"formula_{index:03d}"
            image_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.png"
            crop = crop_region(
                pdf_path,
                provenance=item.get("provenance") or [],
                destination=image_path,
                scale=self.settings.formula_crop_scale,
                padding_points=self.settings.formula_crop_padding_points,
            )
            image_asset: dict[str, Any] | None = None
            if crop.get("status") == "saved":
                image_asset = {
                    "path": relative_posix(image_path, package_dir),
                    "width": crop.get("width"),
                    "height": crop.get("height"),
                    "size_bytes": crop.get("size_bytes"),
                    "page": crop.get("page"),
                    "source": "docling_provenance_crop",
                }
            else:
                warnings.append(f"{stem}: {crop.get('error')}")

            parser_candidates = [
                value
                for value in [
                    item.get("text"),
                    item.get("original"),
                    grobid_item.get("text") if grobid_item else None,
                ]
                if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip()
            ]
            latex = str(item.get("text") or "").strip() or (
                str(grobid_item.get("text") or "").strip() if grobid_item else ""
            )
            plain_text = str(item.get("original") or "").strip() or (
                str(grobid_item.get("text") or "").strip() if grobid_item else latex
            )
            uncertain = not latex or "�" in latex or len(latex) < 2
            repair: dict[str, Any] | None = None
            if (
                uncertain
                and llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_repair_uncertain_formulas
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    repair = await self.llm.transcribe_formula(
                        image_path,
                        parser_candidates=parser_candidates,
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                    repaired_latex = str(repair.get("latex") or "").strip()
                    if repaired_latex:
                        latex = repaired_latex
                        plain_text = str(repair.get("plain_text") or plain_text)
                except Exception as exc:
                    warnings.append(f"{stem}: LLM formula transcription failed: {exc}")

            description = self._description_record(
                repair,
                element_type="formula",
            )
            if (
                description is None
                and llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_describe_all_elements
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    raw_description = await self.llm.describe_formula(
                        image_path,
                        latex=latex or None,
                        plain_text=plain_text or None,
                        parser_candidates=parser_candidates,
                    )
                    description = self._description_record(
                        raw_description,
                        element_type="formula",
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                except Exception as exc:
                    warnings.append(f"{stem}: LLM formula description failed: {exc}")

            description_assets = self._write_description_assets(
                output_dir=output_dir,
                package_dir=package_dir,
                stem=stem,
                description=description,
            )
            tex_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.tex"
            text_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.txt"
            atomic_write_text(tex_path, latex + ("\n" if latex else ""))
            atomic_write_text(text_path, plain_text + ("\n" if plain_text else ""))
            combined_latex.append(f"% {stem}\n\\[{latex}\\]\n" if latex else f"% {stem}: missing\n")
            region = region_from_docling(item.get("provenance") or [])
            formula_quality_status = (
                "repaired" if repair and latex else "accepted" if latex else "failed"
            )

            output.append(
                {
                    "id": stem,
                    "index": index,
                    "equation_number": grobid_item.get("label") if grobid_item else None,
                    "latex": latex or None,
                    "text": plain_text or None,
                    "description": description,
                    "original": item.get("original"),
                    "page": region.page if region else None,
                    "quality": {
                        "status": formula_quality_status,
                        "llm_repair": repair,
                        "parser_candidates": parser_candidates,
                    },
                    "provenance": {
                        "docling": item.get("provenance") or [],
                        "grobid_coords": grobid_item.get("coords") if grobid_item else None,
                    },
                    "alignment": {
                        "matched": grobid_item is not None,
                        "confidence": round(match_score, 4),
                        "components": match_parts,
                        "grobid_id": grobid_item.get("id") if grobid_item else None,
                    },
                    "assets": {
                        "image": image_asset,
                        "latex": relative_posix(tex_path, package_dir),
                        "text": relative_posix(text_path, package_dir),
                        **description_assets,
                    },
                    "sources": {
                        "docling_id": item.get("id"),
                        "grobid": grobid_item,
                    },
                }
            )

        for grobid_index, grobid_item in enumerate(grobid_formulas):
            if grobid_index in used_grobid:
                continue
            index = len(output) + 1
            stem = f"formula_{index:03d}"
            image_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.png"
            crop = crop_grobid_region(
                pdf_path,
                coords=grobid_item.get("coords"),
                destination=image_path,
                scale=self.settings.formula_crop_scale,
                padding_points=self.settings.formula_crop_padding_points,
            )
            image_asset = None
            if crop.get("status") == "saved":
                image_asset = {
                    "path": relative_posix(image_path, package_dir),
                    "width": crop.get("width"),
                    "height": crop.get("height"),
                    "size_bytes": crop.get("size_bytes"),
                    "page": crop.get("page"),
                    "source": "grobid_provenance_crop",
                }
            else:
                warnings.append(f"{stem}: {crop.get('error')}")

            plain_text = str(grobid_item.get("text") or "").strip()
            latex = plain_text
            repair = None
            if (
                llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_repair_uncertain_formulas
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    repair = await self.llm.transcribe_formula(
                        image_path, parser_candidates=[plain_text] if plain_text else []
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                    repaired_latex = str(repair.get("latex") or "").strip()
                    if repaired_latex:
                        latex = repaired_latex
                        plain_text = str(repair.get("plain_text") or plain_text)
                except Exception as exc:
                    warnings.append(f"{stem}: LLM formula transcription failed: {exc}")

            parser_candidates = [plain_text] if plain_text else []
            description = self._description_record(
                repair,
                element_type="formula",
            )
            if (
                description is None
                and llm_available
                and self.settings.llm_describe_all_elements
                and llm_used < llm_budget
                and image_path.exists()
            ):
                try:
                    raw_description = await self.llm.describe_formula(
                        image_path,
                        latex=latex or None,
                        plain_text=plain_text or None,
                        parser_candidates=parser_candidates,
                    )
                    description = self._description_record(
                        raw_description,
                        element_type="formula",
                    )
                    llm_used += 1
                except Exception as exc:
                    warnings.append(f"{stem}: LLM formula description failed: {exc}")

            description_assets = self._write_description_assets(
                output_dir=output_dir,
                package_dir=package_dir,
                stem=stem,
                description=description,
            )
            tex_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.tex"
            text_path = output_dir / f"{stem}.txt"
            atomic_write_text(tex_path, latex + ("\n" if latex else ""))
            atomic_write_text(text_path, plain_text + ("\n" if plain_text else ""))
            combined_latex.append(f"% {stem}\n\\[{latex}\\]\n" if latex else f"% {stem}: missing\n")
            grobid_regions = regions_from_grobid(grobid_item.get("coords"))
            output.append(
                {
                    "id": stem,
                    "index": index,
                    "equation_number": grobid_item.get("label"),
                    "latex": latex or None,
                    "text": plain_text or None,
                    "description": description,
                    "original": grobid_item.get("text"),
                    "page": grobid_regions[0].page if grobid_regions else None,
                    "quality": {
                        "status": "repaired" if repair and latex else "unverified",
                        "llm_repair": repair,
                        "parser_candidates": [grobid_item.get("text")],
                        "warning": (
                            "GROBID formula text is stored as a LaTeX candidate but was not "
                            "produced by Docling formula enrichment."
                        ),
                    },
                    "provenance": {"docling": [], "grobid_coords": grobid_item.get("coords")},
                    "alignment": {
                        "matched": False,
                        "confidence": 0.0,
                        "components": {},
                        "grobid_id": grobid_item.get("id"),
                        "status": "grobid_only",
                    },
                    "assets": {
                        "image": image_asset,
                        "latex": relative_posix(tex_path, package_dir),
                        "text": relative_posix(text_path, package_dir),
                        **description_assets,
                    },
                    "sources": {"docling_id": None, "grobid": grobid_item},
                }
            )
        return output, warnings, llm_used, "\n".join(combined_latex)

    def _master_latex(
        self,
        figures: list[dict[str, Any]],
        tables: list[dict[str, Any]],
        formulas: list[dict[str, Any]],
    ) -> str:
        lines = [
            r"\documentclass{article}",
            r"\usepackage{graphicx}",
            r"\usepackage{booktabs}",
            r"\usepackage{amsmath}",
            r"\begin{document}",
            r"\input{formulas.tex}",
            r"\input{tables.tex}",
        ]
        if figures:
            lines.append("% Figure assets are relative to the package root.")
            for figure in figures:
                image = ((figure.get("assets") or {}).get("image") or {}).get("path")
                if image:
                    lines.extend(
                        [
                            r"\begin{figure}[htbp]",
                            r"\centering",
                            rf"\includegraphics[width=0.9\linewidth]{{../{image}}}",
                            rf"\caption{{{latex_escape(figure.get('caption') or '')}}}",
                            r"\end{figure}",
                        ]
                    )
        lines.extend([r"\end{document}", ""])
        return "\n".join(lines)


===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/coordinates.py =====

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Region:
    page: int
    left: float
    top: float
    right: float
    bottom: float
    origin: str = "BOTTOMLEFT"

    @property
    def width(self) -> float:
        return max(0.0, self.right - self.left)

    @property
    def height(self) -> float:
        return max(0.0, self.top - self.bottom)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "page": self.page,
            "bbox": {
                "left": self.left,
                "top": self.top,
                "right": self.right,
                "bottom": self.bottom,
                "coord_origin": self.origin,
            },
        }


def region_from_docling(provenance: list[dict[str, Any]] | None) -> Region | None:
    if not provenance:
        return None
    item = provenance[0]
    bbox = item.get("bbox") or {}
    try:
        return Region(
            page=int(item.get("page_no") or 0),
            left=float(bbox["l"]),
            top=float(bbox["t"]),
            right=float(bbox["r"]),
            bottom=float(bbox["b"]),
            origin=str(bbox.get("coord_origin") or "BOTTOMLEFT").upper(),
        )
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def regions_from_grobid(coords: str | None) -> list[Region]:
    if not coords:
        return []
    regions: list[Region] = []
    for fragment in coords.split(";"):
        parts = [part.strip() for part in fragment.split(",")]
        if len(parts) != 5:
            continue
        try:
            page = int(float(parts[0]))
            x = float(parts[1])
            y = float(parts[2])
            width = float(parts[3])
            height = float(parts[4])
        except ValueError:
            continue
        # GROBID coordinates are serialized as page,x,y,width,height. Keep the
        # source convention explicit rather than pretending they are Docling boxes.
        regions.append(
            Region(
                page=page,
                left=x,
                top=y,
                right=x + width,
                bottom=y + height,
                origin="TOPLEFT_XYWH",
            )
        )
    return regions


===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/cropper.py =====

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pymupdf

from app.canonical.coordinates import Region, region_from_docling, regions_from_grobid


def _fitz_rect(page: pymupdf.Page, region: Region, padding: float) -> pymupdf.Rect:
    if region.origin == "BOTTOMLEFT":
        x0 = region.left
        y0 = page.rect.height - region.top
        x1 = region.right
        y1 = page.rect.height - region.bottom
    elif region.origin.startswith("TOPLEFT"):
        x0 = region.left
        y0 = region.top
        x1 = region.right
        y1 = region.bottom
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported coordinate origin: {region.origin}")

    rect = pymupdf.Rect(
        min(x0, x1) - padding,
        min(y0, y1) - padding,
        max(x0, x1) + padding,
        max(y0, y1) + padding,
    )
    return rect & page.rect


def crop_explicit_region(
    pdf_path: Path,
    *,
    region: Region | None,
    destination: Path,
    scale: float,
    padding_points: float,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not region or region.page <= 0:
        return {"status": "not_available", "error": "Missing valid page region."}

    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with pymupdf.open(pdf_path) as document:
        if region.page > document.page_count:
            return {
                "status": "not_available",
                "error": f"Page {region.page} is outside the PDF page range.",
            }
        page = document[region.page - 1]
        clip = _fitz_rect(page, region, padding_points)
        if clip.is_empty or clip.width <= 0 or clip.height <= 0:
            return {"status": "not_available", "error": "Computed crop rectangle is empty."}
        pixmap = page.get_pixmap(
            matrix=pymupdf.Matrix(scale, scale),
            clip=clip,
            alpha=False,
        )
        pixmap.save(destination)
        return {
            "status": "saved",
            "filename": destination.name,
            "width": pixmap.width,
            "height": pixmap.height,
            "size_bytes": destination.stat().st_size,
            "page": region.page,
            "source_region": region.to_dict(),
        }


def crop_region(
    pdf_path: Path,
    *,
    provenance: list[dict[str, Any]] | None,
    destination: Path,
    scale: float,
    padding_points: float,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return crop_explicit_region(
        pdf_path,
        region=region_from_docling(provenance),
        destination=destination,
        scale=scale,
        padding_points=padding_points,
    )


def crop_grobid_region(
    pdf_path: Path,
    *,
    coords: str | None,
    destination: Path,
    scale: float,
    padding_points: float,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    regions = regions_from_grobid(coords)
    return crop_explicit_region(
        pdf_path,
        region=regions[0] if regions else None,
        destination=destination,
        scale=scale,
        padding_points=padding_points,
    )


===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/exporters.py =====

import csv
import html
import io
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.utils import atomic_write_text

_LATEX_ESCAPE = {
    "&": r"\&",
    "%": r"\%",
    "$": r"\$",
    "#": r"\#",
    "_": r"\_",
    "{": r"\{",
    "}": r"\}",
    "~": r"\textasciitilde{}",
    "^": r"\textasciicircum{}",
    "\\": r"\textbackslash{}",
}


def latex_escape(value: object) -> str:
    text = "" if value is None else str(value)
    return "".join(_LATEX_ESCAPE.get(char, char) for char in text)


def table_matrix(table_item: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[list[str], list[list[str]], list[str]]:
    data = table_item.get("data") or {}
    cells = data.get("table_cells") or []
    row_count = int(data.get("num_rows") or 0)
    col_count = int(data.get("num_cols") or 0)
    warnings: list[str] = []

    if row_count <= 0 or col_count <= 0 or not cells:
        return [], [], ["Docling returned an empty table structure."]

    grid = [["" for _ in range(col_count)] for _ in range(row_count)]
    header_rows: set[int] = set()
    for cell in cells:
        try:
            row_start = int(cell.get("start_row_offset_idx") or 0)
            row_end = int(cell.get("end_row_offset_idx") or row_start + 1)
            col_start = int(cell.get("start_col_offset_idx") or 0)
            col_end = int(cell.get("end_col_offset_idx") or col_start + 1)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            warnings.append("Skipped a cell with invalid row or column offsets.")
            continue
        if row_start >= row_count or col_start >= col_count:
            warnings.append("Skipped an out-of-range table cell.")
            continue
        text = " ".join(str(cell.get("text") or "").split())
        grid[row_start][col_start] = text
        if bool(cell.get("column_header")):
            header_rows.add(row_start)
        # Preserve span information without duplicating text into every cell.
        if row_end - row_start > 1 or col_end - col_start > 1:
            warnings.append(
                f"Cell at row {row_start}, column {col_start} spans multiple cells; "
                "the flat matrix stores text only in its top-left position."
            )

    header_index = min(header_rows) if header_rows else 0
    columns = grid[header_index]
    rows = [row for index, row in enumerate(grid) if index != header_index]
    if not any(columns):
        columns = [f"column_{index + 1}" for index in range(col_count)]
        rows = grid
        warnings.append("No explicit column header was found; generated placeholder names.")
    return columns, rows, list(dict.fromkeys(warnings))


def normalize_llm_table(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[list[str], list[list[str]], list[str]]:
    columns = [str(value) for value in payload.get("columns") or []]
    rows = [[str(value) for value in row] for row in payload.get("rows") or []]
    warnings = [str(value) for value in payload.get("warnings") or []]
    width = len(columns)
    if width == 0 and rows:
        width = max(len(row) for row in rows)
        columns = [f"column_{index + 1}" for index in range(width)]
        warnings.append(
            "The vision model returned rows without headers; placeholders were generated."
        )
    normalized_rows: list[list[str]] = []
    for row in rows:
        if len(row) < width:
            row = row + [""] * (width - len(row))
        elif len(row) > width:
            warnings.append("A vision-model row had more cells than headers and was truncated.")
            row = row[:width]
        normalized_rows.append(row)
    return columns, normalized_rows, list(dict.fromkeys(warnings))


def table_to_csv(columns: list[str], rows: list[list[str]]) -> str:
    buffer = io.StringIO(newline="")
    writer = csv.writer(buffer)
    if columns:
        writer.writerow(columns)
    writer.writerows(rows)
    return buffer.getvalue()


def table_to_markdown(columns: list[str], rows: list[list[str]]) -> str:
    if not columns:
        return ""

    def clean(value: str) -> str:
        return value.replace("|", r"\|").replace("\n", " ")

    lines = [
        "| " + " | ".join(clean(value) for value in columns) + " |",
        "| " + " | ".join("---" for _ in columns) + " |",
    ]
    lines.extend("| " + " | ".join(clean(value) for value in row) + " |" for row in rows)
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def table_to_html(columns: list[str], rows: list[list[str]], caption: str | None) -> str:
    parts = ["<table>"]
    if caption:
        parts.append(f"  <caption>{html.escape(caption)}</caption>")
    if columns:
        parts.append("  <thead><tr>")
        parts.extend(f"    <th>{html.escape(value)}</th>" for value in columns)
        parts.append("  </tr></thead>")
    parts.append("  <tbody>")
    for row in rows:
        parts.append("    <tr>")
        parts.extend(f"      <td>{html.escape(value)}</td>" for value in row)
        parts.append("    </tr>")
    parts.extend(["  </tbody>", "</table>"])
    return "\n".join(parts) + "\n"


def table_to_latex(
    columns: list[str],
    rows: list[list[str]],
    *,
    caption: str | None,
    label: str,
) -> str:
    if not columns:
        return "% No structured table data was available.\n"
    alignment = "l" * len(columns)
    lines = [
        r"\begin{table}[htbp]",
        r"\centering",
    ]
    if caption:
        lines.append(rf"\caption{{{latex_escape(caption)}}}")
    lines.extend(
        [
            rf"\label{{{latex_escape(label)}}}",
            rf"\begin{{tabular}}{{{alignment}}}",
            r"\hline",
            " & ".join(latex_escape(value) for value in columns) + r" \\",
            r"\hline",
        ]
    )
    for row in rows:
        lines.append(" & ".join(latex_escape(value) for value in row) + r" \\")
    lines.extend(
        [
            r"\hline",
            r"\end{tabular}",
            r"\end{table}",
            "",
        ]
    )
    return "\n".join(lines)


def write_table_assets(
    directory: Path,
    *,
    stem: str,
    columns: list[str],
    rows: list[list[str]],
    caption: str | None,
    latex_label: str,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    paths = {
        "csv": directory / f"{stem}.csv",
        "markdown": directory / f"{stem}.md",
        "html": directory / f"{stem}.html",
        "latex": directory / f"{stem}.tex",
    }
    atomic_write_text(paths["csv"], table_to_csv(columns, rows))
    atomic_write_text(paths["markdown"], table_to_markdown(columns, rows))
    atomic_write_text(paths["html"], table_to_html(columns, rows, caption))
    atomic_write_text(
        paths["latex"],
        table_to_latex(columns, rows, caption=caption, label=latex_label),
    )
    return {key: path.name for key, path in paths.items()}


===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/matching.py =====

import re
from collections.abc import Callable
from difflib import SequenceMatcher
from typing import Any

from app.canonical.coordinates import region_from_docling, regions_from_grobid

_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"[^a-z0-9]+")


def normalize_text(value: str | None) -> str:
    return _TOKEN_RE.sub(" ", (value or "").lower()).strip()


def text_similarity(left: str | None, right: str | None) -> float:
    a = normalize_text(left)
    b = normalize_text(right)
    if not a or not b:
        return 0.0
    return SequenceMatcher(None, a, b).ratio()


def first_page_from_docling(item: dict[str, Any]) -> int | None:
    region = region_from_docling(item.get("provenance") or item.get("prov") or [])
    return region.page if region else None


def first_page_from_grobid(item: dict[str, Any]) -> int | None:
    regions = regions_from_grobid(item.get("coords"))
    return regions[0].page if regions else None


def best_match(
    source: dict[str, Any],
    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]],
    *,
    source_text: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], str | None],
    candidate_text: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], str | None],
    source_page: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], int | None] = first_page_from_docling,
    candidate_page: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], int | None] = first_page_from_grobid,
    used_indexes: set[int] | None = None,
    source_index: int | None = None,
) -> tuple[int | None, float, dict[str, float]]:
    used_indexes = used_indexes or set()
    best_index: int | None = None
    best_score = -1.0
    best_parts: dict[str, float] = {}

    for index, candidate in enumerate(candidates):
        if index in used_indexes:
            continue

        similarity = text_similarity(source_text(source), candidate_text(candidate))
        left_page = source_page(source)
        right_page = candidate_page(candidate)
        page_score = 0.0
        if left_page is not None and right_page is not None:
            page_score = (
                1.0
                if left_page == right_page
                else max(
                    0.0,
                    1.0 - 0.5 * abs(left_page - right_page),
                )
            )

        order_score = 0.0
        if source_index is not None:
            order_score = max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.25 * abs(source_index - index))

        score = (0.60 * similarity) + (0.25 * page_score) + (0.15 * order_score)
        if score > best_score:
            best_index = index
            best_score = score
            best_parts = {
                "text_similarity": round(similarity, 4),
                "page_score": round(page_score, 4),
                "order_score": round(order_score, 4),
            }

    if best_score < 0.25:
        return None, max(best_score, 0.0), best_parts
    return best_index, best_score, best_parts


===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/quality.py =====

from typing import Any

from app.config import Settings


def build_quality_report(
    canonical: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    extraction: dict[str, Any],
    settings: Settings,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    issues: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    score = 1.0

    docling_status = (extraction.get("docling") or {}).get("status") or "failed"
    grobid_status = (extraction.get("grobid") or {}).get("status") or "failed"
    if docling_status != "completed":
        issues.append(
            {
                "severity": "critical",
                "type": "docling_failed",
                "message": "Docling extraction did not complete.",
            }
        )
        score -= 0.45
    if grobid_status != "completed":
        issues.append(
            {
                "severity": "warning",
                "type": "grobid_failed",
                "message": (
                    "GROBID extraction did not complete; scholarly metadata may be incomplete."
                ),
            }
        )
        score -= 0.20

    metadata = canonical.get("metadata") or {}
    for field in settings.metadata_required_fields:
        if not metadata.get(field):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "warning",
                    "type": "missing_metadata",
                    "field": field,
                    "message": f"Required metadata field '{field}' is missing.",
                }
            )
            score -= 0.05

    content = canonical.get("content") or {}
    text_blocks = content.get("text_blocks") or []
    if not text_blocks:
        issues.append(
            {
                "severity": "critical",
                "type": "missing_text",
                "message": "No canonical text blocks were produced.",
            }
        )
        score -= 0.30

    sections = content.get("sections") or []
    paragraphs = content.get("paragraphs") or []
    if not sections:
        issues.append(
            {
                "severity": "warning",
                "type": "missing_section_hierarchy",
                "message": "No article section hierarchy could be constructed.",
            }
        )
        score -= 0.05
    section_ids = {section.get("id") for section in sections}
    for section in sections:
        parent_id = section.get("parent_id")
        if parent_id and parent_id not in section_ids:
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "warning",
                    "type": "invalid_section_parent",
                    "element_id": section.get("id"),
                    "message": "A section points to a parent that is not present.",
                }
            )
            score -= 0.025
    if sections and not paragraphs:
        issues.append(
            {
                "severity": "info",
                "type": "section_paragraphs_missing",
                "message": "Sections were found but no section-linked paragraphs were produced.",
            }
        )

    tables = content.get("tables") or []
    for table in tables:
        quality = table.get("quality") or {}
        if quality.get("status") == "failed":
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "warning",
                    "type": "table_failed",
                    "element_id": table.get("id"),
                    "message": "A detected table has no usable row/column structure.",
                }
            )
            score -= 0.05
        elif quality.get("status") == "repaired":
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "info",
                    "type": "table_repaired_by_llm",
                    "element_id": table.get("id"),
                    "message": (
                        "An empty table structure was reconstructed by the configured vision model."
                    ),
                }
            )
        if not ((table.get("assets") or {}).get("image")):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "info",
                    "type": "table_image_missing",
                    "element_id": table.get("id"),
                    "message": "A detected table has no local image asset.",
                }
            )

    formulas = (canonical.get("content") or {}).get("formulas") or []
    for formula in formulas:
        if not formula.get("latex"):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "warning",
                    "type": "formula_missing_latex",
                    "element_id": formula.get("id"),
                    "message": "A formula has no LaTeX representation.",
                }
            )
            score -= 0.025
        if (formula.get("quality") or {}).get("status") == "unverified":
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "info",
                    "type": "formula_latex_unverified",
                    "element_id": formula.get("id"),
                    "message": (
                        "The formula was found only by GROBID; its text is retained as an "
                        "unverified LaTeX candidate."
                    ),
                }
            )
        if not ((formula.get("assets") or {}).get("image")):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "info",
                    "type": "formula_image_missing",
                    "element_id": formula.get("id"),
                    "message": "A formula image crop could not be generated.",
                }
            )

    figures = (canonical.get("content") or {}).get("figures") or []
    for figure in figures:
        if not figure.get("caption"):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "info",
                    "type": "figure_caption_missing",
                    "element_id": figure.get("id"),
                    "message": "A detected figure has no parser caption.",
                }
            )
        if not ((figure.get("assets") or {}).get("image")):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "warning",
                    "type": "figure_image_missing",
                    "element_id": figure.get("id"),
                    "message": "A detected figure has no local image asset.",
                }
            )
            score -= 0.025

    llm_info = (canonical.get("provenance") or {}).get("llm") or {}
    llm_descriptions_expected = bool(
        llm_info.get("requested_for_document")
        and llm_info.get("available")
        and settings.llm_describe_all_elements
    )
    for element_type in ("figures", "tables", "formulas"):
        for element in content.get(element_type) or []:
            alignment = element.get("alignment") or {}
            confidence = float(alignment.get("confidence") or 0.0)
            if alignment.get("matched") and confidence < 0.45:
                issues.append(
                    {
                        "severity": "info",
                        "type": "low_cross_parser_alignment",
                        "element_type": element_type,
                        "element_id": element.get("id"),
                        "confidence": confidence,
                        "message": "Docling/GROBID alignment confidence is low.",
                    }
                )
            if sections and not element.get("section_id"):
                issues.append(
                    {
                        "severity": "info",
                        "type": "element_section_unresolved",
                        "element_type": element_type,
                        "element_id": element.get("id"),
                        "message": "The element could not be linked to an article section.",
                    }
                )
            if (
                llm_descriptions_expected
                and ((element.get("assets") or {}).get("image"))
                and not element.get("description")
            ):
                issues.append(
                    {
                        "severity": "info",
                        "type": "llm_description_missing",
                        "element_type": element_type,
                        "element_id": element.get("id"),
                        "message": "An image asset exists but no Llama description was generated.",
                    }
                )

    docling_counts = ((extraction.get("docling") or {}).get("parsed") or {}).get("counts") or {}
    grobid_counts = ((extraction.get("grobid") or {}).get("parsed") or {}).get("counts") or {}
    for key in ("tables", "formulas"):
        left = int(docling_counts.get(key) or 0)
        right = int(grobid_counts.get(key) or 0)
        if max(left, right) > 0 and abs(left - right) > max(1, int(0.25 * max(left, right))):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "severity": "info",
                    "type": "cross_parser_count_mismatch",
                    "element_type": key,
                    "docling_count": left,
                    "grobid_count": right,
                    "message": f"Docling and GROBID disagree on the number of {key}.",
                }
            )

    score = round(max(0.0, min(1.0, score)), 4)
    if score >= settings.quality_green_threshold and not any(
        issue["severity"] == "critical" for issue in issues
    ):
        status = "green"
    elif score >= settings.quality_yellow_threshold:
        status = "yellow"
    else:
        status = "red"

    return {
        "status": status,
        "score": score,
        "thresholds": {
            "green": settings.quality_green_threshold,
            "yellow": settings.quality_yellow_threshold,
        },
        "issue_count": len(issues),
        "issues": issues,
        "parser_status": {
            "docling": docling_status,
            "grobid": grobid_status,
        },
    }


===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/refs.py =====

from typing import Any


def ref_value(value: object) -> str | None:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for key in ("$ref", "cref", "ref"):
            candidate = value.get(key)
            if isinstance(candidate, str):
                return candidate
    return None


def resolve_ref(document: dict[str, Any], reference: object) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    value = ref_value(reference)
    if not value or not value.startswith("#/"):
        return None

    node: object = document
    for part in value[2:].split("/"):
        if isinstance(node, dict):
            node = node.get(part)
        elif isinstance(node, list) and part.isdigit():
            index = int(part)
            if index >= len(node):
                return None
            node = node[index]
        else:
            return None
    return node if isinstance(node, dict) else None


def referenced_texts(document: dict[str, Any], references: list[object] | None) -> list[str]:
    values: list[str] = []
    for reference in references or []:
        node = resolve_ref(document, reference)
        if not node:
            continue
        value = node.get("text") or node.get("orig")
        if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
            values.append(" ".join(value.split()))
    return values


===== FILE: agent/app/canonical/sections.py =====

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections import defaultdict
from difflib import SequenceMatcher
from typing import Any
from xml.etree import ElementTree as ET

TEI_NS = "http://www.tei-c.org/ns/1.0"
XML_NS = "http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace"
NS = {"tei": TEI_NS}


def _tag(name: str) -> str:
    return f"{{{TEI_NS}}}{name}"


def _clean(value: str | None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    cleaned = " ".join(value.split())
    return cleaned or None


def _node_text(node: ET.Element | None) -> str | None:
    if node is None:
        return None
    return _clean(" ".join(node.itertext()))


def _normalize(value: str | None) -> str:
    if not value:
        return ""
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", value.casefold()).strip()


def _pages_from_coords(coords: str | None) -> list[int]:
    pages: list[int] = []
    if not coords:
        return pages
    for region in coords.split(";"):
        first = region.strip().split(",", 1)[0].strip()
        try:
            page = int(float(first))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        if page > 0 and page not in pages:
            pages.append(page)
    return pages


def _infer_number_and_title(title: str | None, number: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
    clean_title = _clean(title)
    clean_number = _clean(number)
    if clean_number or not clean_title:
        return clean_number, clean_title
    match = re.match(r"^\s*((?:\d+\.)*\d+)\s*[.)]?\s+(.+)$", clean_title)
    if not match:
        return None, clean_title
    return match.group(1), _clean(match.group(2))


def _level_from_number(number: str | None) -> int:
    if not number:
        return 1
    components = [part for part in re.split(r"[.\-]", number) if part]
    return max(1, len(components))


def _new_section(
    *,
    index: int,
    source_id: str | None,
    title: str | None,
    number: str | None,
    level: int,
    parent_id: str | None,
    path: list[str],
    head_coords: str | None,
    source: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    pages = _pages_from_coords(head_coords)
    return {
        "id": f"sec_{index:03d}",
        "source_id": source_id,
        "title": title,
        "heading": title,
        "number": number,
        "level": level,
        "parent_id": parent_id,
        "path": path,
        "child_section_ids": [],
        "paragraph_ids": [],
        "figure_ids": [],
        "table_ids": [],
        "formula_ids": [],
        "text": "",
        "order": index,
        "page_start": min(pages) if pages else None,
        "page_end": max(pages) if pages else None,
        "provenance": {
            "source": source,
            "head_coords": head_coords,
        },
    }


def _finalize_ranges_and_text(
    sections: list[dict[str, Any]],
    paragraphs: list[dict[str, Any]],
) -> None:
    paragraph_by_id = {paragraph["id"]: paragraph for paragraph in paragraphs}
    section_by_id = {section["id"]: section for section in sections}

    for section in sections:
        section_paragraphs = [
            paragraph_by_id[paragraph_id]
            for paragraph_id in section.get("paragraph_ids") or []
            if paragraph_id in paragraph_by_id
        ]
        section["text"] = "\n\n".join(
            paragraph["text"] for paragraph in section_paragraphs if paragraph.get("text")
        )
        pages = [
            page
            for paragraph in section_paragraphs
            for page in (paragraph.get("page_start"), paragraph.get("page_end"))
            if isinstance(page, int)
        ]
        if pages:
            section["page_start"] = min(
                [section["page_start"]] + pages
                if isinstance(section.get("page_start"), int)
                else pages
            )
            section["page_end"] = max(
                [section["page_end"]] + pages if isinstance(section.get("page_end"), int) else pages
            )

    for section in sorted(sections, key=lambda value: int(value.get("level") or 1), reverse=True):
        parent_id = section.get("parent_id")
        if not parent_id or parent_id not in section_by_id:
            continue
        parent = section_by_id[parent_id]
        child_pages = [
            page
            for page in (section.get("page_start"), section.get("page_end"))
            if isinstance(page, int)
        ]
        if child_pages:
            existing = [
                page
                for page in (parent.get("page_start"), parent.get("page_end"))
                if isinstance(page, int)
            ]
            all_pages = existing + child_pages
            parent["page_start"] = min(all_pages)
            parent["page_end"] = max(all_pages)


def _parse_tei_structure(tei_xml: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    root = ET.fromstring(tei_xml)
    body = root.find(".//tei:text/tei:body", NS)
    if body is None:
        return {
            "sections": [],
            "paragraphs": [],
            "source_element_to_section": {},
            "source": "grobid_tei",
        }

    sections: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    paragraphs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    source_element_to_section: dict[str, str] = {}
    paragraph_index = 0

    def map_source_elements(node: ET.Element, section_id: str) -> None:
        for child in list(node):
            if child.tag == _tag("div"):
                continue
            for descendant in child.iter():
                if descendant.tag not in {_tag("figure"), _tag("formula")}:
                    continue
                source_id = descendant.get(f"{{{XML_NS}}}id")
                if source_id:
                    source_element_to_section[source_id] = section_id

    def visit_div(
        div: ET.Element,
        *,
        parent_id: str | None,
        parent_path: list[str],
        level: int,
    ) -> None:
        nonlocal paragraph_index

        head = div.find("./tei:head", NS)
        number, title = _infer_number_and_title(
            _node_text(head),
            head.get("n") if head is not None else None,
        )
        direct_paragraphs = [
            paragraph for paragraph in div.findall("./tei:p", NS) if _node_text(paragraph)
        ]
        nested_divs = div.findall("./tei:div", NS)

        # GROBID occasionally emits wrapper divs without a heading or direct text.
        # Preserve the real nested sections instead of creating a meaningless node.
        if not title and not direct_paragraphs and nested_divs:
            for nested in nested_divs:
                visit_div(
                    nested,
                    parent_id=parent_id,
                    parent_path=parent_path,
                    level=level,
                )
            return

        section_index = len(sections) + 1
        section_title = title or "Untitled section"
        path = [*parent_path, section_title]
        section = _new_section(
            index=section_index,
            source_id=div.get(f"{{{XML_NS}}}id"),
            title=section_title,
            number=number,
            level=level,
            parent_id=parent_id,
            path=path,
            head_coords=head.get("coords") if head is not None else None,
            source="grobid_tei",
        )
        sections.append(section)
        if parent_id:
            parent = next((item for item in sections if item["id"] == parent_id), None)
            if parent is not None:
                parent["child_section_ids"].append(section["id"])

        map_source_elements(div, section["id"])

        for paragraph in direct_paragraphs:
            text = _node_text(paragraph)
            if not text:
                continue
            paragraph_index += 1
            coords = paragraph.get("coords")
            pages = _pages_from_coords(coords)
            paragraph_id = f"para_{paragraph_index:05d}"
            paragraph_record = {
                "id": paragraph_id,
                "source_id": paragraph.get(f"{{{XML_NS}}}id"),
                "section_id": section["id"],
                "section_path": path,
                "order": paragraph_index,
                "text": text,
                "page_start": min(pages) if pages else None,
                "page_end": max(pages) if pages else None,
                "provenance": {
                    "source": "grobid_tei",
                    "coords": coords,
                },
            }
            paragraphs.append(paragraph_record)
            section["paragraph_ids"].append(paragraph_id)

            for descendant in paragraph.iter():
                if descendant.tag not in {_tag("figure"), _tag("formula")}:
                    continue
                source_id = descendant.get(f"{{{XML_NS}}}id")
                if source_id:
                    source_element_to_section[source_id] = section["id"]

        for nested in nested_divs:
            visit_div(
                nested,
                parent_id=section["id"],
                parent_path=path,
                level=level + 1,
            )

    direct_body_paragraphs = [
        paragraph for paragraph in body.findall("./tei:p", NS) if _node_text(paragraph)
    ]
    if direct_body_paragraphs:
        section = _new_section(
            index=1,
            source_id=None,
            title="Body",
            number=None,
            level=1,
            parent_id=None,
            path=["Body"],
            head_coords=None,
            source="grobid_tei_synthetic",
        )
        sections.append(section)
        for paragraph in direct_body_paragraphs:
            paragraph_index += 1
            text = _node_text(paragraph)
            if not text:
                continue
            coords = paragraph.get("coords")
            pages = _pages_from_coords(coords)
            paragraph_id = f"para_{paragraph_index:05d}"
            paragraphs.append(
                {
                    "id": paragraph_id,
                    "source_id": paragraph.get(f"{{{XML_NS}}}id"),
                    "section_id": section["id"],
                    "section_path": ["Body"],
                    "order": paragraph_index,
                    "text": text,
                    "page_start": min(pages) if pages else None,
                    "page_end": max(pages) if pages else None,
                    "provenance": {
                        "source": "grobid_tei",
                        "coords": coords,
                    },
                }
            )
            section["paragraph_ids"].append(paragraph_id)

    for div in body.findall("./tei:div", NS):
        visit_div(div, parent_id=None, parent_path=[], level=1)

    _finalize_ranges_and_text(sections, paragraphs)
    return {
        "sections": sections,
        "paragraphs": paragraphs,
        "source_element_to_section": source_element_to_section,
        "source": "grobid_tei",
    }


def _fallback_structure(flat_sections: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    sections: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    paragraphs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    stack: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    paragraph_index = 0

    for source_index, source in enumerate(flat_sections, start=1):
        number, title = _infer_number_and_title(
            source.get("heading") or source.get("title"),
            source.get("number"),
        )
        level = _level_from_number(number)
        while stack and int(stack[-1].get("level") or 1) >= level:
            stack.pop()
        parent = stack[-1] if stack else None
        parent_path = list(parent.get("path") or []) if parent else []
        section_title = title or f"Untitled section {source_index}"
        section = _new_section(
            index=len(sections) + 1,
            source_id=source.get("id"),
            title=section_title,
            number=number,
            level=level,
            parent_id=parent.get("id") if parent else None,
            path=[*parent_path, section_title],
            head_coords=source.get("coords"),
            source="grobid_flat_fallback",
        )
        sections.append(section)
        if parent:
            parent["child_section_ids"].append(section["id"])
        stack.append(section)

        for value in source.get("paragraphs") or []:
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                continue
            paragraph_index += 1
            paragraph_id = f"para_{paragraph_index:05d}"
            paragraphs.append(
                {
                    "id": paragraph_id,
                    "source_id": None,
                    "section_id": section["id"],
                    "section_path": list(section["path"]),
                    "order": paragraph_index,
                    "text": " ".join(value.split()),
                    "page_start": None,
                    "page_end": None,
                    "provenance": {
                        "source": "grobid_flat_fallback",
                        "coords": None,
                    },
                }
            )
            section["paragraph_ids"].append(paragraph_id)

    _finalize_ranges_and_text(sections, paragraphs)
    return {
        "sections": sections,
        "paragraphs": paragraphs,
        "source_element_to_section": {},
        "source": "grobid_flat_fallback",
    }


def build_section_structure(
    *,
    tei_xml: str | None,
    flat_sections: list[dict[str, Any]] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if tei_xml and tei_xml.strip():
        try:
            parsed = _parse_tei_structure(tei_xml)
            if parsed["sections"]:
                return parsed
        except ET.ParseError:
            pass
    return _fallback_structure(flat_sections or [])


def build_section_tree(sections: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    by_id: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    roots: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for section in sections:
        node = {key: value for key, value in section.items() if key not in {"child_section_ids"}}
        node["children"] = []
        by_id[str(section["id"])] = node

    for section in sections:
        node = by_id[str(section["id"])]
        parent_id = section.get("parent_id")
        if parent_id and str(parent_id) in by_id:
            by_id[str(parent_id)]["children"].append(node)
        else:
            roots.append(node)
    return roots


def _best_title_match(title: str, sections: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    normalized = _normalize(title)
    if not normalized:
        return None
    best: tuple[float, dict[str, Any] | None] = (0.0, None)
    for section in sections:
        candidate = _normalize(section.get("title"))
        if not candidate:
            continue
        score = (
            1.0 if normalized == candidate else SequenceMatcher(None, normalized, candidate).ratio()
        )
        if score > best[0]:
            best = (score, section)
    return best[1] if best[0] >= 0.72 else None


def assign_text_blocks_to_sections(
    text_blocks: list[dict[str, Any]],
    *,
    sections: list[dict[str, Any]],
    paragraphs: list[dict[str, Any]],
) -> None:
    if not sections:
        return

    paragraph_candidates: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for paragraph in paragraphs:
        normalized = _normalize(paragraph.get("text"))
        if normalized:
            paragraph_candidates[normalized].append(paragraph)

    section_by_id = {section["id"]: section for section in sections}
    current_section: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    used_paragraphs: set[str] = set()

    for block in text_blocks:
        label = str(block.get("label") or "").casefold()
        text = str(block.get("text") or "")
        if "section" in label and "header" in label:
            matched_section = _best_title_match(text, sections)
            if matched_section:
                current_section = matched_section

        paragraph_match: dict[str, Any] | None = None
        normalized = _normalize(text)
        for candidate in paragraph_candidates.get(normalized, []):
            if candidate["id"] not in used_paragraphs:
                paragraph_match = candidate
                break
        if paragraph_match is None and normalized:
            for paragraph in paragraphs:
                if paragraph["id"] in used_paragraphs:
                    continue
                candidate = _normalize(paragraph.get("text"))
                if not candidate:
                    continue
                if len(normalized) >= 40 and (normalized in candidate or candidate in normalized):
                    paragraph_match = paragraph
                    break

        if paragraph_match:
            used_paragraphs.add(paragraph_match["id"])
            current_section = section_by_id.get(paragraph_match["section_id"], current_section)
            block["paragraph_id"] = paragraph_match["id"]

        if current_section:
            block["section_id"] = current_section["id"]
            block["section_title"] = current_section.get("title")
            block["section_path"] = list(current_section.get("path") or [])


def _section_for_page(page: int | None, sections: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if page is None:
        return None
    within = [
        section
        for section in sections
        if isinstance(section.get("page_start"), int)
        and isinstance(section.get("page_end"), int)
        and int(section["page_start"]) <= page <= int(section["page_end"])
    ]
    if within:
        return max(
            within,
            key=lambda value: (
                int(value.get("level") or 1),
                int(value.get("order") or 0),
            ),
        )
    before = [
        section
        for section in sections
        if isinstance(section.get("page_start"), int) and int(section["page_start"]) <= page
    ]
    if before:
        return max(
            before,
            key=lambda value: (
                int(value.get("page_start") or 0),
                int(value.get("level") or 1),
                int(value.get("order") or 0),
            ),
        )
    return None


def attach_elements_to_sections(
    *,
    sections: list[dict[str, Any]],
    source_element_to_section: dict[str, str],
    figures: list[dict[str, Any]],
    tables: list[dict[str, Any]],
    formulas: list[dict[str, Any]],
) -> None:
    section_by_id = {section["id"]: section for section in sections}

    def attach(elements: list[dict[str, Any]], bucket: str) -> None:
        for element in elements:
            grobid = (element.get("sources") or {}).get("grobid") or {}
            grobid_id = grobid.get("id")
            section_id = source_element_to_section.get(str(grobid_id)) if grobid_id else None
            section = section_by_id.get(section_id) if section_id else None
            method = "grobid_tei_element_id" if section is not None else None
            confidence = 1.0 if section is not None else 0.0
            if section is None:
                page = element.get("page")
                section = _section_for_page(int(page) if isinstance(page, int) else None, sections)
                if section is not None:
                    method = "section_page_range"
                    confidence = 0.55
            if section is None:
                element["section_id"] = None
                element["section_title"] = None
                element["section_path"] = []
                element["section_assignment"] = {
                    "method": "unresolved",
                    "confidence": 0.0,
                }
                continue
            element["section_id"] = section["id"]
            element["section_title"] = section.get("title")
            element["section_path"] = list(section.get("path") or [])
            element["section_assignment"] = {
                "method": method,
                "confidence": confidence,
            }
            section.setdefault(bucket, []).append(element["id"])

    attach(figures, "figure_ids")
    attach(tables, "table_ids")
    attach(formulas, "formula_ids")


===== FILE: agent/app/cli.py =====

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
from pathlib import Path

from app.config import get_settings
from app.pipeline import AgentPipeline


async def _run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    settings = get_settings()
    pipeline = AgentPipeline(settings)
    try:
        result = await pipeline.process(
            pdf_path=Path(args.pdf).resolve(),
            filename=Path(args.pdf).name,
            use_llm=args.use_llm,
            output_folder=args.output_folder,
            overwrite=args.overwrite,
        )
    finally:
        await pipeline.close()

    summary = {
        "document_id": result["document_id"],
        "output_folder": result["package_name"],
        "package_dir": str(result["package_dir"]),
        "canonical_json": str(result["canonical_path"]),
        "zip": str(result["zip_path"]) if result["zip_path"] else None,
        "quality": result["canonical"].get("quality"),
        "counts": result["canonical"].get("counts"),
        "processing": result.get("processing"),
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Process a PDF through the extraction tools and build a canonical package."
    )
    parser.add_argument("pdf", help="Path to the PDF file")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--use-llm",
        action="store_true",
        help="Enable the configured bounded Ollama/Llama fallback.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-folder",
        help="Safe folder name to create below the configured output root.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--overwrite",
        action="store_true",
        help="Replace an existing package with the same output folder name.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    path = Path(args.pdf)
    if not path.is_file():
        parser.error(f"PDF does not exist: {path}")
    return asyncio.run(_run(args))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
