===== FILE: agent/app/clients/extractor.py =====

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx

from app.config import Settings
from app.utils import join_url


class ExtractorClient:
    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=settings.tools_base_url.rstrip("/"),
            timeout=httpx.Timeout(settings.tools_timeout_seconds),
            verify=settings.tools_verify_tls,
            follow_redirects=True,
        )

    async def close(self) -> None:
        await self._client.aclose()

    async def is_ready(self) -> bool:
        try:
            response = await self._client.get(self.settings.tools_ready_path)
            return response.status_code == 200
        except httpx.HTTPError:
            return False

    async def extract(
        self,
        pdf_path: Path,
        *,
        filename: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        data = {
            "include_docling_markdown": "true",
            "include_docling_json": "true",
            "include_grobid_tei": "true",
            "export_picture_images": "true",
            "export_table_images": "true",
            "grobid_consolidate_header": "0",
            "grobid_consolidate_citations": "0",
            "grobid_include_raw_citations": "true",
            "grobid_include_raw_affiliations": "true",
            "grobid_segment_sentences": "true",
        }
        with pdf_path.open("rb") as file_handle:
            files = {"file": (filename, file_handle, "application/pdf")}
            response = await self._client.post(
                self.settings.tools_extract_path,
                data=data,
                files=files,
            )
        response.raise_for_status()
        payload = response.json()
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise ValueError("Extractor response is not a JSON object.")
        return payload

    async def download_asset(self, asset_url: str, destination: Path) -> None:
        url = asset_url
        if asset_url.startswith("/"):
            url = join_url(self.settings.tools_base_url, asset_url)
        destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        async with self._client.stream("GET", url) as response:
            response.raise_for_status()
            with destination.open("wb") as output:
                async for chunk in response.aiter_bytes():
                    output.write(chunk)


===== FILE: agent/app/clients/ollama.py =====

import base64
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx

from app.config import Settings


class OllamaClient:
    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=settings.ollama_base_url.rstrip("/"),
            timeout=httpx.Timeout(settings.ollama_timeout_seconds),
            follow_redirects=True,
        )

    async def close(self) -> None:
        await self._client.aclose()

    async def is_alive(self) -> bool:
        try:
            response = await self._client.get("/api/tags")
            return response.status_code == 200
        except httpx.HTTPError:
            return False

    async def _vision_json(
        self,
        *,
        image_path: Path,
        prompt: str,
        schema: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        encoded = base64.b64encode(image_path.read_bytes()).decode("ascii")
        payload = {
            "model": self.settings.ollama_model,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": prompt,
                    "images": [encoded],
                }
            ],
            "format": schema,
            "stream": False,
            "keep_alive": self.settings.ollama_keep_alive,
            "options": {"temperature": self.settings.ollama_temperature},
        }
        response = await self._client.post("/api/chat", json=payload)
        response.raise_for_status()
        body = response.json()
        content = ((body.get("message") or {}).get("content") or "").strip()
        parsed = json.loads(content)
        if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
            raise ValueError("Ollama structured response is not a JSON object.")
        return parsed

    async def describe_figure(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        caption: str | None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        schema = {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "description": {"type": "string"},
                "summary": {"type": "string"},
                "figure_type": {"type": "string"},
                "visible_text": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "key_observations": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "confidence": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
                "warnings": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
            },
            "required": [
                "description",
                "summary",
                "figure_type",
                "visible_text",
                "key_observations",
                "confidence",
                "warnings",
            ],
        }
        prompt = (
            "Analyze this scientific-article figure. Describe only visible evidence and the "
            "relationship between visible components. Do not invent values, causes, or "
            "conclusions. "
            "Identify whether it is a chart, diagram, photo, plot, workflow, or other figure. "
            f"The parser caption is: {caption or '[missing]'} ."
        )
        return await self._vision_json(image_path=image_path, prompt=prompt, schema=schema)

    async def describe_table(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        caption: str | None,
        columns: list[str],
        rows: list[list[str]],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        schema = {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "description": {"type": "string"},
                "summary": {"type": "string"},
                "key_observations": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "visible_headers": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "confidence": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
                "warnings": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
            },
            "required": [
                "description",
                "summary",
                "key_observations",
                "visible_headers",
                "confidence",
                "warnings",
            ],
        }
        prompt = (
            "Describe this scientific table using only information visible in the image. "
            "Explain what is compared or reported, identify the visible headers, and summarize "
            "obvious patterns only when directly supported by the cells. Do not calculate, infer "
            "missing values, or claim statistical significance unless explicitly shown. "
            f"Parser caption: {caption or '[missing]'}. "
            f"Parser columns: {columns}. Parser row count: {len(rows)}."
        )
        return await self._vision_json(image_path=image_path, prompt=prompt, schema=schema)

    async def repair_table(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        caption: str | None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        schema = {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "columns": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "rows": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                },
                "notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "description": {"type": "string"},
                "summary": {"type": "string"},
                "key_observations": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "confidence": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
                "warnings": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
            },
            "required": [
                "columns",
                "rows",
                "notes",
                "description",
                "summary",
                "key_observations",
                "confidence",
                "warnings",
            ],
        }
        prompt = (
            "Transcribe this scientific table exactly. Preserve column order and values. "
            "Use an empty string for unreadable cells and report uncertainty in warnings. "
            "Do not calculate or infer missing numbers. Also provide a grounded description and "
            "brief summary of what the visible table reports. "
            f"The parser caption is: {caption or '[missing]'} ."
        )
        return await self._vision_json(image_path=image_path, prompt=prompt, schema=schema)

    async def describe_formula(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        latex: str | None,
        plain_text: str | None,
        parser_candidates: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        schema = {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "description": {"type": "string"},
                "summary": {"type": "string"},
                "visible_structure": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "symbols": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "properties": {
                            "symbol": {"type": "string"},
                            "visible_role": {"type": "string"},
                        },
                        "required": ["symbol", "visible_role"],
                    },
                },
                "confidence": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
                "warnings": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
            },
            "required": [
                "description",
                "summary",
                "visible_structure",
                "symbols",
                "confidence",
                "warnings",
            ],
        }
        prompt = (
            "Describe the visible mathematical formula without inventing domain meaning that is "
            "not shown. "
            "Explain its visible structure, operators, terms, indices, fractions, matrices, "
            "or delimiters. A symbol's role should be visual/syntactic unless its meaning is "
            "explicit. "
            f"Current LaTeX candidate: {latex or '[missing]'}. "
            f"Plain-text candidate: {plain_text or '[missing]'}. "
            f"Other parser candidates: {parser_candidates}."
        )
        return await self._vision_json(image_path=image_path, prompt=prompt, schema=schema)

    async def transcribe_formula(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        parser_candidates: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        schema = {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "latex": {"type": "string"},
                "plain_text": {"type": "string"},
                "description": {"type": "string"},
                "summary": {"type": "string"},
                "visible_structure": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "symbols": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "properties": {
                            "symbol": {"type": "string"},
                            "visible_role": {"type": "string"},
                        },
                        "required": ["symbol", "visible_role"],
                    },
                },
                "confidence": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
                "warnings": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
            },
            "required": [
                "latex",
                "plain_text",
                "description",
                "summary",
                "visible_structure",
                "symbols",
                "confidence",
                "warnings",
            ],
        }
        prompt = (
            "Transcribe the displayed mathematical formula into valid LaTeX. Preserve subscripts, "
            "superscripts, Greek letters, delimiters, operators, matrices, and equation structure. "
            "Do not add explanatory prose to the LaTeX field. Also provide a grounded description "
            "of the visible mathematical structure without inventing domain meaning. "
            f"Parser candidates: {parser_candidates}."
        )
        return await self._vision_json(image_path=image_path, prompt=prompt, schema=schema)


===== FILE: agent/app/config.py =====

from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        case_sensitive=False,
        extra="ignore",
    )

    app_name: str = "PDF Canonicalization Agent"
    app_version: str = "0.3.0"
    log_level: str = "INFO"
    max_upload_size_mb: int = Field(default=100, gt=0, le=2000)
    max_concurrency: int = Field(default=1, gt=0, le=16)

    tools_base_url: str = "http://host.docker.internal:8090"
    tools_extract_path: str = "/v1/extract"
    tools_ready_path: str = "/health/ready"
    tools_timeout_seconds: float = Field(default=1800.0, gt=1)
    tools_verify_tls: bool = True

    output_dir: Path = Path("/data/outputs")
    packages_url_prefix: str = "/packages"
    keep_raw_extractor_response: bool = False
    overwrite_existing_package: bool = False
    create_zip_package: bool = True

    formula_crop_scale: float = Field(default=3.0, ge=1.0, le=8.0)
    formula_crop_padding_points: float = Field(default=8.0, ge=0.0, le=100.0)
    fallback_asset_crop_scale: float = Field(default=2.5, ge=1.0, le=8.0)
    fallback_asset_crop_padding_points: float = Field(default=5.0, ge=0.0, le=100.0)

    llm_enabled: bool = False
    llm_provider: Literal["ollama"] = "ollama"
    ollama_base_url: str = "http://host.docker.internal:11434"
    ollama_model: str = "llama3.2-vision:11b"
    ollama_timeout_seconds: float = Field(default=600.0, gt=1)
    ollama_keep_alive: str = "10m"
    ollama_temperature: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=2.0)
    llm_validate_all_figures: bool = False
    llm_describe_all_elements: bool = True
    llm_repair_empty_tables: bool = True
    llm_repair_uncertain_formulas: bool = True
    llm_max_items_per_document: int = Field(default=500, ge=0, le=5000)

    metadata_required_fields: tuple[str, ...] = ("title",)
    quality_green_threshold: float = Field(default=0.90, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    quality_yellow_threshold: float = Field(default=0.65, ge=0.0, le=1.0)

    @property
    def max_upload_size_bytes(self) -> int:
        return self.max_upload_size_mb * 1024 * 1024


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()


===== FILE: agent/app/main.py =====

import logging
import re
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, File, Form, HTTPException, Request, UploadFile, status
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from app.config import get_settings
from app.models import ProcessResponse
from app.pipeline import AgentPipeline
from app.utils import (
    join_url,
    safe_filename,
    safe_output_folder,
    save_upload_to_temp,
    validate_output_folder,
)

settings = get_settings()
logging.basicConfig(
    level=getattr(logging, settings.log_level.upper(), logging.INFO),
    format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_DOCUMENT_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-f0-9]{64}$")


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    settings.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    app.state.pipeline = AgentPipeline(settings)
    yield
    await app.state.pipeline.close()


settings.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.app_name,
    version=settings.app_version,
    description=(
        "Calls the PDF extraction tools service, aligns Docling and GROBID, performs "
        "deterministic quality checks, optionally invokes a bounded Llama vision fallback, "
        "and writes a canonical document package."
    ),
    lifespan=lifespan,
)
app.mount(
    settings.packages_url_prefix,
    StaticFiles(directory=str(settings.output_dir)),
    name="packages",
)


@app.get("/health/live")
async def live() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"status": "ok"}


@app.get("/health/ready")
async def ready(request: Request) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result = await request.app.state.pipeline.ready()
    if not result["ready"]:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail={"status": "not_ready", **result},
        )
    return {"status": "ready", **result}


@app.get("/v1/runtime")
async def runtime(request: Request) -> dict[str, Any]:
    readiness = await request.app.state.pipeline.ready()
    return {
        "app": {"name": settings.app_name, "version": settings.app_version},
        "tools": {
            "base_url": settings.tools_base_url,
            "ready": readiness["tools_ready"],
        },
        "llm": {
            "enabled": settings.llm_enabled,
            "provider": settings.llm_provider,
            "base_url": settings.ollama_base_url if settings.llm_enabled else None,
            "model": settings.ollama_model if settings.llm_enabled else None,
            "alive": readiness["llm_alive"],
        },
        "output_dir": str(settings.output_dir),
    }


@app.post("/v1/process", response_model=ProcessResponse)
async def process_pdf(
    request: Request,
    file: Annotated[UploadFile, File(description="Scientific article PDF")],
    use_llm: Annotated[bool, Form()] = False,
    output_folder: Annotated[str | None, Form()] = None,
    overwrite: Annotated[bool | None, Form()] = None,
) -> ProcessResponse:
    filename = safe_filename(file.filename)
    if Path(filename).suffix.lower() != ".pdf":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_415_UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE,
            detail="Only PDF uploads are supported.",
        )

    normalized_output_folder: str | None = None
    if output_folder is not None:
        try:
            normalized_output_folder = safe_output_folder(output_folder)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

    temp_path: Path | None = None
    try:
        temp_path, _sha256, _size = await save_upload_to_temp(
            file,
            max_bytes=settings.max_upload_size_bytes,
        )
        result = await request.app.state.pipeline.process(
            pdf_path=temp_path,
            filename=filename,
            use_llm=use_llm,
            output_folder=normalized_output_folder,
            overwrite=overwrite,
        )
    except FileExistsError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Canonical PDF processing failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    finally:
        if temp_path is not None:
            temp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)

    canonical = result["canonical"]
    document_id = result["document_id"]
    package_name = result["package_name"]
    package_dir = result["package_dir"]
    canonical_path = result["canonical_path"]
    zip_path = result["zip_path"]
    quality = canonical.get("quality") or {}
    warnings = canonical.get("warnings") or []
    response_status = "completed" if quality.get("status") == "green" else "completed_with_warnings"

    package_url = join_url(settings.packages_url_prefix, f"{package_name}/")
    canonical_url = join_url(
        settings.packages_url_prefix,
        f"{package_name}/canonical_document.json",
    )
    zip_url = join_url(settings.packages_url_prefix, f"{package_name}.zip") if zip_path else None

    return ProcessResponse(
        status=response_status,
        document_id=document_id,
        package_name=package_name,
        output_folder=package_name,
        package_directory=str(package_dir),
        canonical_json=str(canonical_path),
        zip_file=str(zip_path) if zip_path else None,
        urls={
            "assets_base": package_url,
            "canonical_json": canonical_url,
            "zip": zip_url,
            "download": f"/v1/packages/{package_name}/download" if zip_path else None,
        },
        quality=quality,
        counts=canonical.get("counts") or {},
        warnings=warnings,
        processing=result.get("processing") or {},
    )


@app.get("/v1/documents/{document_id}")
async def get_document(document_id: str) -> FileResponse:
    if not _DOCUMENT_ID_RE.fullmatch(document_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid document ID.")
    path = settings.output_dir / document_id / "canonical_document.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Document package not found.")
    return FileResponse(path, media_type="application/json", filename=path.name)


@app.get("/v1/documents/{document_id}/download")
async def download_document(document_id: str) -> FileResponse:
    if not _DOCUMENT_ID_RE.fullmatch(document_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid document ID.")
    path = settings.output_dir / f"{document_id}.zip"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Document ZIP package not found.")
    return FileResponse(path, media_type="application/zip", filename=path.name)


@app.get("/v1/packages/{package_name}")
async def get_package(package_name: str) -> FileResponse:
    try:
        safe_name = validate_output_folder(package_name)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    path = settings.output_dir / safe_name / "canonical_document.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Document package not found.")
    return FileResponse(path, media_type="application/json", filename=path.name)


@app.get("/v1/packages/{package_name}/download")
async def download_package(package_name: str) -> FileResponse:
    try:
        safe_name = validate_output_folder(package_name)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    path = settings.output_dir / f"{safe_name}.zip"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Document ZIP package not found.")
    return FileResponse(path, media_type="application/zip", filename=path.name)

from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class ProcessResponse(BaseModel):
    schema_version: str = "0.3.0"
    status: Literal["completed", "completed_with_warnings", "failed"]
    document_id: str
    package_name: str
    output_folder: str
    package_directory: str
    canonical_json: str
    zip_file: str | None = None
    urls: dict[str, str | None] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    quality: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    counts: dict[str, int] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    warnings: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    processing: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


===== FILE: agent/app/pipeline.py =====

import asyncio
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.canonical.builder import CanonicalPackageBuilder
from app.clients.extractor import ExtractorClient
from app.clients.ollama import OllamaClient
from app.config import Settings


class AgentPipeline:
    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self.extractor = ExtractorClient(settings)
        self.llm = OllamaClient(settings)
        self.builder = CanonicalPackageBuilder(
            settings,
            extractor=self.extractor,
            llm=self.llm,
        )
        self._semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(settings.max_concurrency)

    async def close(self) -> None:
        await self.extractor.close()
        await self.llm.close()

    async def ready(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        tools_ready = await self.extractor.is_ready()
        llm_alive = await self.llm.is_alive() if self.settings.llm_enabled else None
        return {
            "tools_ready": tools_ready,
            "llm_enabled": self.settings.llm_enabled,
            "llm_alive": llm_alive,
            "ready": tools_ready,
        }

    async def process(
        self,
        *,
        pdf_path: Path,
        filename: str,
        use_llm: bool,
        output_folder: str | None = None,
        overwrite: bool | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        async with self._semaphore:
            started = time.perf_counter()
            extraction_started = time.perf_counter()
            extraction = await self.extractor.extract(pdf_path, filename=filename)
            extraction_seconds = time.perf_counter() - extraction_started

            packaging_started = time.perf_counter()
            result = await self.builder.build(
                pdf_path=pdf_path,
                filename=filename,
                extraction=extraction,
                use_llm=use_llm,
                output_folder=output_folder,
                overwrite=overwrite,
            )
            packaging_seconds = time.perf_counter() - packaging_started
            result["processing"] = {
                "extraction_seconds": round(extraction_seconds, 3),
                "canonicalization_seconds": round(packaging_seconds, 3),
                "total_seconds": round(time.perf_counter() - started, 3),
            }
            return result


===== FILE: agent/app/utils.py =====

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, BinaryIO

from fastapi import UploadFile

_SAFE_FILENAME_RE = re.compile(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+")
_SAFE_OUTPUT_FOLDER_RE = re.compile(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+")
_MAX_OUTPUT_FOLDER_LENGTH = 100
_WINDOWS_RESERVED_OUTPUT_NAMES = {
    "CON",
    "PRN",
    "AUX",
    "NUL",
    *(f"COM{index}" for index in range(1, 10)),
    *(f"LPT{index}" for index in range(1, 10)),
}


def safe_filename(filename: str | None) -> str:
    candidate = Path(filename or "document.pdf").name
    candidate = _SAFE_FILENAME_RE.sub("_", candidate).strip("._")
    return candidate or "document.pdf"


def safe_output_folder(value: str) -> str:
    """Return a single safe output-directory name.

    The value is a name, not a path. Path separators and traversal tokens are
    rejected so callers can never escape the configured output root.
    """

    normalized = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", value).strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise ValueError("Output folder name cannot be empty.")
    if normalized in {".", ".."} or "/" in normalized or "\\" in normalized:
        raise ValueError("Output folder must be a name, not a path.")

    candidate = _SAFE_OUTPUT_FOLDER_RE.sub("_", normalized).strip("._-")
    candidate = candidate[:_MAX_OUTPUT_FOLDER_LENGTH].rstrip("._-")
    if not candidate or candidate in {".", ".."}:
        raise ValueError("Output folder name contains no usable characters.")
    if candidate.upper() in _WINDOWS_RESERVED_OUTPUT_NAMES:
        raise ValueError("Output folder name is reserved by Windows.")
    return candidate


def validate_output_folder(value: str) -> str:
    """Validate that a URL path component is already in canonical safe form."""

    candidate = safe_output_folder(value)
    if candidate != value:
        raise ValueError("Invalid output folder name.")
    return candidate


async def save_upload_to_temp(
    upload: UploadFile,
    *,
    max_bytes: int,
) -> tuple[Path, str, int]:
    suffix = Path(upload.filename or "document.pdf").suffix.lower() or ".pdf"
    fd, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix="pdf-agent-", suffix=suffix)
    os.close(fd)
    path = Path(temporary_name)
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    size = 0

    try:
        with path.open("wb") as output:
            while chunk := await upload.read(1024 * 1024):
                size += len(chunk)
                if size > max_bytes:
                    raise ValueError(f"Upload exceeds the {max_bytes} byte limit.")
                digest.update(chunk)
                output.write(chunk)
    except Exception:
        path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    finally:
        await upload.close()

    return path, digest.hexdigest(), size


def copy_stream_and_hash(
    source: BinaryIO,
    destination: Path,
    *,
    max_bytes: int,
) -> tuple[str, int]:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    size = 0
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with destination.open("wb") as output:
        while chunk := source.read(1024 * 1024):
            size += len(chunk)
            if size > max_bytes:
                destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                raise ValueError(f"Input exceeds the {max_bytes} byte limit.")
            digest.update(chunk)
            output.write(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest(), size


def atomic_write_json(path: Path, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    temporary.write_text(
        json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    temporary.replace(path)


def atomic_write_text(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    temporary.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
    temporary.replace(path)


def recreate_directory(path: Path) -> None:
    if path.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(path)
    path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def join_url(base: str, path: str) -> str:
    return f"{base.rstrip('/')}/{path.lstrip('/')}"


def relative_posix(path: Path, root: Path) -> str:
    return path.relative_to(root).as_posix()

===== FILE: agent/docker-entrypoint.sh =====

#!/bin/sh
set -eu

mkdir -p /data/outputs

TARGET_UID="${HOST_UID:-10002}"
TARGET_GID="${HOST_GID:-10002}"

case "$TARGET_UID:$TARGET_GID" in
  *[!0-9:]*|:*|*:) 
    echo "HOST_UID and HOST_GID must be numeric; using image defaults." >&2
    TARGET_UID=10002
    TARGET_GID=10002
    ;;
esac

CURRENT_UID="$(id -u appuser)"
CURRENT_GID="$(id -g appuser)"

if [ "$TARGET_GID" != "$CURRENT_GID" ]; then
  groupmod -o -g "$TARGET_GID" appuser
fi

if [ "$TARGET_UID" != "$CURRENT_UID" ]; then
  usermod -o -u "$TARGET_UID" appuser
fi

chown appuser:appuser /data/outputs

exec gosu appuser "$@"

pydantic-settings==2.14.2

===== FILE: agent/scripts/smoke_test.sh =====

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

PDF_PATH="${1:-../tools/tests/fixtures/scientific_sample.pdf}"
AGENT_URL="${AGENT_URL:-http://localhost:8100}"
RESULT_FILE="${RESULT_FILE:-/tmp/pdf-agent-smoke-result.json}"

curl --fail --show-error \
  -X POST "${AGENT_URL}/v1/process" \
  -F "file=@${PDF_PATH};type=application/pdf" \
  -F "use_llm=false" \
  -o "${RESULT_FILE}"

jq . "${RESULT_FILE}"
DOCUMENT_ID=$(jq -r .document_id "${RESULT_FILE}")
CANONICAL="data/outputs/${DOCUMENT_ID}/canonical_document.json"

test -f "${CANONICAL}"
jq -e '.schema_version == "1.2.0"' "${CANONICAL}" >/dev/null
jq -e '.quality.status | IN("green", "yellow", "red")' "${CANONICAL}" >/dev/null

echo "Smoke test passed: ${CANONICAL}"


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_builder.py =====

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pymupdf
import pytest

from app.canonical.builder import CanonicalPackageBuilder
from app.config import Settings


class FakeExtractor:
    async def download_asset(self, asset_url: str, destination: Path) -> None:
        raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected asset download: {asset_url} -> {destination}")


class FakeLlm:
    async def is_alive(self) -> bool:
        return False


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_builder_creates_canonical_package(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    pdf_path = tmp_path / "article.pdf"
    pdf = pymupdf.open()
    page = pdf.new_page(width=600, height=800)
    page.insert_text((60, 80), "Sample Paper", fontsize=18)
    page.insert_text((60, 200), "E = mc^2", fontsize=16)
    page.draw_rect(pymupdf.Rect(60, 300, 300, 400))
    page.insert_text((70, 325), "Model   Accuracy", fontsize=12)
    page.insert_text((70, 355), "A       0.91", fontsize=12)
    page.draw_rect(pymupdf.Rect(350, 300, 550, 450))
    page.insert_text((365, 330), "Figure content", fontsize=12)
    pdf.save(pdf_path)
    pdf.close()

    extraction: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "0.2.0",
        "request_id": "request-1",
        "status": "completed",
        "document": {
            "filename": "article.pdf",
            "size_bytes": pdf_path.stat().st_size,
            "sha256": "a" * 64,
        },
        "warnings": [],
        "docling": {
            "status": "completed",
            "version": "test",
            "stats": {"tables": 1, "pictures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            "raw": {
                "markdown": "# Sample Paper\n\nBody text.\n",
                "document_json": {
                    "name": "Sample Paper",
                    "texts": [
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/texts/0",
                            "label": "section_header",
                            "text": "Sample Paper",
                            "orig": "Sample Paper",
                            "prov": [

                                    },
                                }
                            ],
                        },
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/texts/1",
                            "label": "text",
                            "text": "Body text.",
                            "orig": "Body text.",
                            "prov": [],
                        },
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/texts/2",
                            "label": "caption",
                            "text": "Table 1. Results",
                            "orig": "Table 1. Results",
                            "prov": [],
                        },
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/texts/3",
                            "label": "caption",
                            "text": "Figure 1. Architecture",
                            "orig": "Figure 1. Architecture",
                            "prov": [],
                        },
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/texts/4",

                            ],
                        },

                    "tables": [

                            "self_ref": "#/tables/0",
                            "captions": [{"$ref": "#/texts/2"}],
                            "prov": [
                                {
                                    "page_no": 1,

                                        "l": 60,

                                        "text": "A",

                                    },
                                    {
                                        "text": "0.91",
                                        "start_row_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "end_row_offset_idx": 2,
                                        "start_col_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "end_col_offset_idx": 2,
                                    },
                                ],
                            },
                        }
                    ],
                    "pictures": [
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/pictures/0",

                        }

                },
            },
            "parsed": {
                "formulas": [
                    {
                        "id": "#/texts/4",
                        "text": "E = mc^2",
                        "original": "E = mc²",
                        "provenance": [
                            {
                                "page_no": 1,
                                "bbox": {
                                    "l": 50,
                                    "t": 630,
                                    "r": 180,
                                    "b": 570,
                                    "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                },
                            }
                        ],
                    }
                ],
                "tables": [
                    {
                        "id": "#/tables/0",
                        "captions": ["Table 1. Results"],
                        "provenance": [
                            {
                                "page_no": 1,
                                "bbox": {
                                    "l": 60,
                                    "t": 500,
                                    "r": 300,
                                    "b": 400,
                                    "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                },
                            }
                        ],
                        "structure_status": "structured",
                        "asset": None,
                    }
                ],
                "pictures": [
                    {
                        "id": "#/pictures/0",
                        "captions": ["Figure 1. Architecture"],
                        "provenance": [
                            {
                                "page_no": 1,
                                "bbox": {
                                    "l": 350,
                                    "t": 500,
                                    "r": 550,
                                    "b": 350,
                                    "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                },
                            }
                        ],
                        "asset": None,
                    }
                ],
                "counts": {"tables": 1, "pictures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            },
        },
        "grobid": {
            "status": "completed",
            "version": "0.8.2",
            "stats": {"tables": 1, "figures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            "parsed": {
                "metadata": {
                    "title": "Sample Paper",
                    "authors": [{"full_name": "A. Author"}],
                    "abstract": "Abstract.",
                },
                "sections": [
                    {"id": "section-1", "heading": "Introduction", "paragraphs": ["Body text."]}
                ],
                "references": [],
                "figures_and_tables": [
                    {
                        "id": "table-1",
                        "type": "table",
                        "label": "Table 1",
                        "caption": "Results",
                        "coords": "1,60,300,240,100",
                    },
                    {
                        "id": "figure-1",
                        "type": "figure",
                        "label": "Figure 1",
                        "caption": "Architecture",
                        "coords": "1,350,300,200,150",
                    },
                ],
                "formulas": [
                    {
                        "id": "formula-1",
                        "label": "1",
                        "text": "E = mc^2",
                        "coords": "1,50,170,130,60",
                    }
                ],
                "counts": {"tables": 1, "figures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            },
        },
    }

    settings = Settings(
        _env_file=None,
        output_dir=tmp_path / "outputs",
        create_zip_package=False,
        keep_raw_extractor_response=False,
    )
    builder = CanonicalPackageBuilder(
        settings,
        extractor=FakeExtractor(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        llm=FakeLlm(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    result = await builder.build(
        pdf_path=pdf_path,
        filename="article.pdf",
        extraction=extraction,
        use_llm=False,
    )

    package = result["package_dir"]
    canonical = result["canonical"]
    assert (package / "canonical_document.json").is_file()
    assert (package / "figures/figure_001.png").is_file()
    assert (package / "tables/table_001.csv").is_file()
    assert (package / "formulas/formula_001.png").is_file()
    assert canonical["metadata"]["title"] == "Sample Paper"
    assert canonical["content"]["tables"][0]["columns"] == ["Model", "Accuracy"]
    assert canonical["content"]["formulas"][0]["latex"] == "E = mc^2"
    assert canonical["counts"]["figures"] == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_builder_retains_grobid_only_elements(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    pdf_path = tmp_path / "grobid-only.pdf"
    pdf = pymupdf.open()
    page = pdf.new_page(width=400, height=500)
    page.insert_text((40, 80), "GROBID only", fontsize=16)
    page.insert_text((40, 160), "x = y + 1", fontsize=14)
    page.draw_rect(pymupdf.Rect(40, 220, 220, 300))
    page.draw_rect(pymupdf.Rect(240, 220, 380, 360))
    pdf.save(pdf_path)
    pdf.close()

    extraction: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "0.2.0",
        "request_id": "request-2",
        "status": "partial",
        "document": {
            "filename": "grobid-only.pdf",
            "size_bytes": pdf_path.stat().st_size,
            "sha256": "b" * 64,
        },
        "warnings": ["Docling failed"],
        "docling": {
            "status": "failed",
            "version": None,
            "stats": {},
            "raw": None,
            "parsed": None,
        },
        "grobid": {
            "status": "completed",
            "version": "0.8.2",
            "stats": {"tables": 1, "figures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            "parsed": {
                "metadata": {"title": "GROBID only", "authors": []},
                "sections": [
                    {"id": "s1", "heading": "Body", "paragraphs": ["Fallback paragraph."]}
                ],
                "references": [],
                "figures_and_tables": [
                    {
                        "id": "t1",
                        "type": "table",
                        "label": "Table 1",
                        "caption": "Only table",
                        "content": "A B 1 2",
                        "coords": "1,40,220,180,80",
                    },
                    {
                        "id": "f1",
                        "type": "figure",
                        "label": "Figure 1",
                        "caption": "Only figure",
                        "coords": "1,240,220,140,140",
                    },
                ],
                "formulas": [
                    {
                        "id": "eq1",
                        "label": "1",
                        "text": "x = y + 1",
                        "coords": "1,40,130,120,50",
                    }
                ],
                "counts": {"tables": 1, "figures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            },
        },
    }

    settings = Settings(
        _env_file=None,
        output_dir=tmp_path / "outputs",
        create_zip_package=False,
    )
    builder = CanonicalPackageBuilder(
        settings,
        extractor=FakeExtractor(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        llm=FakeLlm(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    result = await builder.build(
        pdf_path=pdf_path,
        filename="grobid-only.pdf",
        extraction=extraction,
        use_llm=False,
    )
    canonical = result["canonical"]
    assert canonical["content"]["text_blocks"][0]["source"] == "grobid_fallback"
    assert canonical["counts"]["figures"] == 1
    assert canonical["counts"]["tables"] == 1
    assert canonical["counts"]["formulas"] == 1
    assert canonical["content"]["formulas"][0]["quality"]["status"] == "unverified"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_builder_uses_custom_output_folder_and_protects_existing_package(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    pdf_path = tmp_path / "custom.pdf"
    pdf = pymupdf.open()
    pdf.new_page(width=200, height=200)
    pdf.save(pdf_path)
    pdf.close()

    extraction: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "0.2.0",
        "request_id": "custom-output",
        "status": "completed",
        "document": {
            "filename": "custom.pdf",
            "size_bytes": pdf_path.stat().st_size,
            "sha256": "c" * 64,
        },
        "warnings": [],
        "docling": {
            "status": "completed",
            "version": "test",
            "stats": {},
            "raw": {"document_json": {"texts": []}},
            "parsed": {},
        },
        "grobid": {
            "status": "completed",
            "version": "test",
            "stats": {},
            "raw": {"tei_xml": None},
            "parsed": {"metadata": {}, "sections": [], "references": []},
        },
    }

    settings = Settings(
        _env_file=None,
        output_dir=tmp_path / "outputs",
        create_zip_package=False,
        overwrite_existing_package=False,
    )
    builder = CanonicalPackageBuilder(
        settings,
        extractor=FakeExtractor(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        llm=FakeLlm(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )

    result = await builder.build(
        pdf_path=pdf_path,
        filename="custom.pdf",
        extraction=extraction,
        use_llm=False,
        output_folder="VRX_case_001",
    )

    assert result["package_name"] == "VRX_case_001"
    assert result["package_dir"] == settings.output_dir / "VRX_case_001"
    assert result["canonical"]["output"]["folder_name"] == "VRX_case_001"

    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        await builder.build(
            pdf_path=pdf_path,
            filename="custom.pdf",
            extraction=extraction,
            use_llm=False,
            output_folder="VRX_case_001",
        )

===== FILE: agent/tests/test_cropper.py =====

from pathlib import Path

import pymupdf

from app.canonical.cropper import crop_region


def test_crop_region_from_bottom_left_coordinates(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    pdf_path = tmp_path / "sample.pdf"
    document = pymupdf.open()
    page = document.new_page(width=300, height=400)
    page.insert_text((50, 100), "E = mc^2", fontsize=20)
    document.save(pdf_path)
    document.close()

    output = tmp_path / "formula.png"
    result = crop_region(
        pdf_path,
        provenance=[
            {
                "page_no": 1,
                "bbox": {
                    "l": 40,
                    "t": 330,
                    "r": 180,
                    "b": 270,
                    "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                },
            }
        ],
        destination=output,
        scale=2.0,
        padding_points=2.0,
    )
    assert result["status"] == "saved"
    assert output.is_file()
    assert result["width"] > 0
    assert result["height"] > 0


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_exporters.py =====

from app.canonical.exporters import (
    normalize_llm_table,
    table_matrix,
    table_to_latex,
    table_to_markdown,
)


def test_table_matrix_extracts_headers_and_rows() -> None:
    table = {
        "data": {
            "num_rows": 3,
            "num_cols": 2,
            "table_cells": [
                {
                    "text": "Model",
                    "start_row_offset_idx": 0,
                    "end_row_offset_idx": 1,
                    "start_col_offset_idx": 0,
                    "end_col_offset_idx": 1,
                    "column_header": True,
                },
                {
                    "text": "Accuracy",
                    "start_row_offset_idx": 0,
                    "end_row_offset_idx": 1,
                    "start_col_offset_idx": 1,
                    "end_col_offset_idx": 2,
                    "column_header": True,
                },
                {
                    "text": "A",
                    "start_row_offset_idx": 1,
                    "end_row_offset_idx": 2,
                    "start_col_offset_idx": 0,
                    "end_col_offset_idx": 1,
                },
                {
                    "text": "0.91",
                    "start_row_offset_idx": 1,
                    "end_row_offset_idx": 2,
                    "start_col_offset_idx": 1,
                    "end_col_offset_idx": 2,
                },
                {
                    "text": "B",
                    "start_row_offset_idx": 2,
                    "end_row_offset_idx": 3,
                    "start_col_offset_idx": 0,
                    "end_col_offset_idx": 1,
                },
                {
                    "text": "0.94",
                    "start_row_offset_idx": 2,
                    "end_row_offset_idx": 3,
                    "start_col_offset_idx": 1,
                    "end_col_offset_idx": 2,
                },
            ],
        }
    }
    columns, rows, warnings = table_matrix(table)
    assert columns == ["Model", "Accuracy"]
    assert rows == [["A", "0.91"], ["B", "0.94"]]
    assert warnings == []
    assert "| Model | Accuracy |" in table_to_markdown(columns, rows)
    assert r"\begin{tabular}{ll}" in table_to_latex(
        columns,
        rows,
        caption="Results",
        label="tab:results",
    )


def test_empty_table_and_llm_normalization() -> None:
    assert table_matrix({"data": {"num_rows": 0, "num_cols": 0}})[0] == []
    columns, rows, warnings = normalize_llm_table(
        {"columns": ["A", "B"], "rows": [["1"], ["2", "3", "4"]]}
    )
    assert columns == ["A", "B"]
    assert rows == [["1", ""], ["2", "3"]]
    assert warnings


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_llm_descriptions.py =====

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pymupdf
import pytest

from app.canonical.builder import CanonicalPackageBuilder
from app.config import Settings


class FakeExtractor:
    async def download_asset(self, asset_url: str, destination: Path) -> None:
        raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected asset download: {asset_url} -> {destination}")


class FakeLlm:
    async def is_alive(self) -> bool:
        return True

    async def describe_figure(self, image_path: Path, *, caption: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "description": "A visible workflow diagram.",
            "summary": "Workflow diagram.",
            "figure_type": "diagram",
            "visible_text": [],
            "key_observations": ["Two connected blocks are visible."],
            "confidence": 0.9,
            "warnings": [],
        }

    async def describe_table(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        caption: str | None,
        columns: list[str],
        rows: list[list[str]],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "description": "A table comparing model accuracy.",
            "summary": "Model comparison table.",
            "key_observations": ["Model A has an accuracy value of 0.91."],
            "visible_headers": columns,
            "confidence": 0.95,
            "warnings": [],
        }

    async def describe_formula(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        latex: str | None,
        plain_text: str | None,
        parser_candidates: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "description": "An equality between energy and mass times the square of c.",
            "summary": "A single equality.",
            "visible_structure": ["Equality", "Superscript 2"],
            "symbols": [{"symbol": "c", "visible_role": "Squared factor"}],
            "confidence": 0.9,
            "warnings": [],
        }

    async def repair_table(self, image_path: Path, *, caption: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        raise AssertionError("Structured table should not be repaired")

    async def transcribe_formula(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
        *,
        parser_candidates: list[str],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        raise AssertionError("Valid formula should not be retranscribed")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_llm_describes_every_visual_element(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    pdf_path = tmp_path / "article.pdf"
    pdf = pymupdf.open()
    page = pdf.new_page(width=600, height=800)
    page.insert_text((60, 100), "Body", fontsize=12)
    page.insert_text((60, 200), "E = mc^2", fontsize=16)
    page.draw_rect(pymupdf.Rect(60, 300, 300, 400))
    page.insert_text((70, 325), "Model Accuracy", fontsize=12)
    page.insert_text((70, 355), "A 0.91", fontsize=12)
    page.draw_rect(pymupdf.Rect(350, 300, 550, 450))
    page.insert_text((365, 330), "Figure", fontsize=12)
    pdf.save(pdf_path)
    pdf.close()

    extraction: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "0.2.0",
        "request_id": "request-llm",
        "status": "completed",
        "document": {
            "filename": "article.pdf",
            "size_bytes": pdf_path.stat().st_size,
            "sha256": "c" * 64,
        },
        "warnings": [],
        "docling": {
            "status": "completed",
            "version": "test",
            "stats": {"tables": 1, "pictures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            "raw": {
                "markdown": "Body\n",
                "document_json": {
                    "name": "Article",
                    "texts": [
                        {"self_ref": "#/texts/0", "label": "text", "text": "Body", "prov": []},
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/texts/1",
                            "label": "formula",
                            "text": "E = mc^2",
                            "orig": "E = mc²",
                            "prov": [
                                {
                                    "page_no": 1,
                                    "bbox": {
                                        "l": 50,
                                        "t": 630,
                                        "r": 190,
                                        "b": 570,
                                        "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                    },
                                }
                            ],
                        },
                    ],
                    "tables": [
                        {
                            "self_ref": "#/tables/0",
                            "captions": [],
                            "prov": [
                                {
                                    "page_no": 1,
                                    "bbox": {
                                        "l": 60,
                                        "t": 500,
                                        "r": 300,
                                        "b": 400,
                                        "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                    },
                                }
                            ],
                            "data": {
                                "num_rows": 2,
                                "num_cols": 2,
                                "table_cells": [
                                    {
                                        "text": "Model",
                                        "start_row_offset_idx": 0,
                                        "end_row_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "start_col_offset_idx": 0,
                                        "end_col_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "column_header": True,
                                    },
                                    {
                                        "text": "Accuracy",
                                        "start_row_offset_idx": 0,
                                        "end_row_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "start_col_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "end_col_offset_idx": 2,
                                        "column_header": True,
                                    },
                                    {
                                        "text": "A",
                                        "start_row_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "end_row_offset_idx": 2,
                                        "start_col_offset_idx": 0,
                                        "end_col_offset_idx": 1,
                                    },
                                    {
                                        "text": "0.91",
                                        "start_row_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "end_row_offset_idx": 2,
                                        "start_col_offset_idx": 1,
                                        "end_col_offset_idx": 2,
                                    },
                                ],
                            },
                        }
                    ],
                    "pictures": [],
                },
            },
            "parsed": {
                "formulas": [
                    {
                        "id": "#/texts/1",
                        "text": "E = mc^2",
                        "original": "E = mc²",
                        "provenance": [
                            {
                                "page_no": 1,
                                "bbox": {
                                    "l": 50,
                                    "t": 630,
                                    "r": 190,
                                    "b": 570,
                                    "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                },
                            }
                        ],
                    }
                ],
                "tables": [
                    {
                        "id": "#/tables/0",
                        "captions": [],
                        "provenance": [
                            {
                                "page_no": 1,
                                "bbox": {
                                    "l": 60,
                                    "t": 500,
                                    "r": 300,
                                    "b": 400,
                                    "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                },
                            }
                        ],
                        "structure_status": "structured",
                        "asset": None,
                    }
                ],
                "pictures": [
                    {
                        "id": "#/pictures/0",
                        "captions": [],
                        "provenance": [
                            {
                                "page_no": 1,
                                "bbox": {
                                    "l": 350,
                                    "t": 500,
                                    "r": 550,
                                    "b": 350,
                                    "coord_origin": "BOTTOMLEFT",
                                },
                            }
                        ],
                        "asset": None,
                    }
                ],
                "counts": {"tables": 1, "pictures": 1, "formulas": 1},
            },
        },
        "grobid": {
            "status": "completed",
            "version": "0.8.2",
            "stats": {},
            "raw": {
                "tei_xml": "<TEI xmlns='http://www.tei-c.org/ns/1.0'><text><body/></text></TEI>"
            },
            "parsed": {
                "metadata": {"title": "Article", "authors": []},
                "sections": [],
                "references": [],
                "figures_and_tables": [],
                "formulas": [],
                "counts": {},
            },
        },
    }

    settings = Settings(
        _env_file=None,
        output_dir=tmp_path / "outputs",
        create_zip_package=False,
        llm_enabled=True,
        llm_describe_all_elements=True,
    )
    builder = CanonicalPackageBuilder(
        settings,
        extractor=FakeExtractor(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        llm=FakeLlm(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    result = await builder.build(
        pdf_path=pdf_path,
        filename="article.pdf",
        extraction=extraction,
        use_llm=True,
    )

    canonical = result["canonical"]
    figure = canonical["content"]["figures"][0]
    table = canonical["content"]["tables"][0]
    formula = canonical["content"]["formulas"][0]
    assert figure["description"]["source"] == "ollama_vision"
    assert table["description"]["text"] == "A table comparing model accuracy."
    assert formula["description"]["summary"] == "A single equality."
    package = result["package_dir"]
    assert (package / "figures/figure_001.description.json").is_file()
    assert (package / "tables/table_001.description.txt").is_file()
    assert (package / "formulas/formula_001.description.json").is_file()
    assert canonical["provenance"]["llm"]["items_processed"] == 3


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_matching.py =====

from app.canonical.matching import best_match, text_similarity


def test_text_similarity_and_best_match() -> None:
    assert text_similarity("Table 2: Model accuracy", "Table 2 Model Accuracy") > 0.95
    source = {
        "captions": ["Performance on the test dataset"],
        "provenance": [{"page_no": 4, "bbox": {"l": 1, "t": 10, "r": 20, "b": 1}}],
    }
    candidates = [
        {"caption": "Unrelated table", "coords": "2,1,1,10,10"},
        {"caption": "Performance on test dataset", "coords": "4,1,1,10,10"},
    ]
    index, score, parts = best_match(
        source,
        candidates,
        source_text=lambda item: " ".join(item.get("captions") or []),
        candidate_text=lambda item: item.get("caption"),
        source_index=1,
    )
    assert index == 1
    assert score > 0.7
    assert parts["page_score"] == 1.0


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_output_folders.py =====

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from app.utils import safe_output_folder, validate_output_folder


def test_safe_output_folder_normalizes_spaces() -> None:
    assert safe_output_folder("VRX analysis 01") == "VRX_analysis_01"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "value",
    ["../escape", "nested/name", r"nested\\name", ".", "..", "   ", "CON", "lpt1"],
)
def test_safe_output_folder_rejects_paths(value: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        safe_output_folder(value)


def test_validate_output_folder_requires_canonical_name() -> None:
    assert validate_output_folder("VRX_analysis-01") == "VRX_analysis-01"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_output_folder("VRX analysis")


def test_custom_output_folder_stays_below_root(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "outputs"
    folder = safe_output_folder("case 001")
    target = (root / folder).resolve()
    assert target.is_relative_to(root.resolve())


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_quality.py =====

from app.canonical.quality import build_quality_report
from app.config import Settings


def test_quality_report_flags_empty_table() -> None:
    canonical = {
        "metadata": {"title": "A paper"},
        "content": {
            "text_blocks": [{"text": "hello"}],
            "tables": [{"id": "table_001", "quality": {"status": "failed"}}],
            "formulas": [],
            "figures": [],
        },
    }
    extraction = {
        "docling": {"status": "completed", "parsed": {"counts": {"tables": 1}}},
        "grobid": {"status": "completed", "parsed": {"counts": {"tables": 1}}},
    }
    report = build_quality_report(canonical, extraction=extraction, settings=Settings())
    assert report["status"] in {"green", "yellow"}
    assert any(issue["type"] == "table_failed" for issue in report["issues"])


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_refs.py =====

from app.canonical.refs import referenced_texts, resolve_ref


def test_resolve_ref_and_caption_text() -> None:
    document = {
        "texts": [
            {"text": "Table 1. Results"},
            {"orig": "Figure caption"},
        ]
    }
    assert resolve_ref(document, {"$ref": "#/texts/0"}) == {"text": "Table 1. Results"}
    assert referenced_texts(
        document,
        [{"$ref": "#/texts/0"}, {"cref": "#/texts/1"}],
    ) == ["Table 1. Results", "Figure caption"]


===== FILE: agent/tests/test_sections.py =====

from app.canonical.sections import (
    assign_text_blocks_to_sections,
    attach_elements_to_sections,
    build_section_structure,
    build_section_tree,
)


def test_nested_tei_sections_and_element_links() -> None:
    tei = """
    <TEI xmlns="http://www.tei-c.org/ns/1.0">
      <text>
        <body>
          <div xml:id="s1">
            <head n="1" coords="1,50,80,200,20">Methods</head>
            <p xml:id="p1" coords="1,50,110,400,60">Overview of the method.</p>
            <div xml:id="s11">
              <head n="1.1" coords="2,50,80,200,20">Dataset</head>
              <p xml:id="p2" coords="2,50,110,400,60">The dataset contains samples.</p>
              <figure xml:id="fig1" coords="2,50,200,300,180">
                <head>Figure 1</head>
                <figDesc>Dataset overview</figDesc>
              </figure>
              <figure xml:id="tab1" type="table" coords="2,50,400,300,100">
                <head>Table 1</head>
                <figDesc>Dataset statistics</figDesc>
              </figure>
              <formula xml:id="eq1" n="1" coords="2,50,550,200,40">x = y + 1</formula>
            </div>
          </div>
        </body>
      </text>
    </TEI>
    """

    structure = build_section_structure(tei_xml=tei, flat_sections=[])
    sections = structure["sections"]
    paragraphs = structure["paragraphs"]

    assert len(sections) == 2
    assert sections[0]["title"] == "Methods"
    assert sections[0]["level"] == 1
    assert sections[1]["title"] == "Dataset"
    assert sections[1]["level"] == 2
    assert sections[1]["parent_id"] == sections[0]["id"]
    assert sections[1]["path"] == ["Methods", "Dataset"]
    assert paragraphs[1]["section_id"] == sections[1]["id"]
    assert structure["source_element_to_section"]["fig1"] == sections[1]["id"]
    assert structure["source_element_to_section"]["tab1"] == sections[1]["id"]
    assert structure["source_element_to_section"]["eq1"] == sections[1]["id"]

    text_blocks = [
        {"id": "b1", "label": "section_header", "text": "Methods"},
        {"id": "b2", "label": "text", "text": "Overview of the method."},
        {"id": "b3", "label": "section_header", "text": "Dataset"},
        {"id": "b4", "label": "text", "text": "The dataset contains samples."},
    ]
    assign_text_blocks_to_sections(
        text_blocks,
        sections=sections,
        paragraphs=paragraphs,
    )
    assert text_blocks[1]["section_path"] == ["Methods"]
    assert text_blocks[3]["section_path"] == ["Methods", "Dataset"]
    assert text_blocks[3]["paragraph_id"] == paragraphs[1]["id"]

    figures = [{"id": "figure_001", "page": 2, "sources": {"grobid": {"id": "fig1"}}}]
    tables = [{"id": "table_001", "page": 2, "sources": {"grobid": {"id": "tab1"}}}]
    formulas = [{"id": "formula_001", "page": 2, "sources": {"grobid": {"id": "eq1"}}}]
    attach_elements_to_sections(
        sections=sections,
        source_element_to_section=structure["source_element_to_section"],
        figures=figures,
        tables=tables,
        formulas=formulas,
    )
    assert figures[0]["section_path"] == ["Methods", "Dataset"]
    assert tables[0]["section_id"] == sections[1]["id"]
    assert formulas[0]["section_title"] == "Dataset"
    assert sections[1]["figure_ids"] == ["figure_001"]
    assert sections[1]["table_ids"] == ["table_001"]
    assert sections[1]["formula_ids"] == ["formula_001"]

    tree = build_section_tree(sections)
    assert len(tree) == 1
    assert tree[0]["title"] == "Methods"
    assert tree[0]["children"][0]["title"] == "Dataset"


def test_flat_section_fallback_infers_subsection_levels() -> None:
    structure = build_section_structure(
        tei_xml=None,
        flat_sections=[
            {"id": "s1", "heading": "Methods", "number": "2", "paragraphs": ["A"]},
            {"id": "s2", "heading": "Dataset", "number": "2.1", "paragraphs": ["B"]},
            {"id": "s3", "heading": "Model", "number": "2.2", "paragraphs": ["C"]},
        ],
    )
    sections = structure["sections"]
    assert sections[1]["parent_id"] == sections[0]["id"]
    assert sections[2]["parent_id"] == sections[0]["id"]
    assert sections[1]["path"] == ["Methods", "Dataset"]

===== FILE: scripts/install-nvidia.sh =====

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$ROOT_DIR"

if ! docker version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "Docker is not available. Install Docker Engine or Docker Desktop first." >&2
  exit 1
fi

if ! docker compose version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "Docker Compose v2 is required." >&2
  exit 1
fi

if ! docker run --rm --gpus all ubuntu nvidia-smi >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "Docker cannot access the NVIDIA GPU. Install/configure NVIDIA Container Toolkit first." >&2
  exit 1
fi

if [[ ! -f .env ]]; then
  cp .env.example .env
fi

mkdir -p outputs

if command -v id >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v awk >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  uid="$(id -u)"
  gid="$(id -g)"
  tmp="$(mktemp)"
  awk -v uid="$uid" -v gid="$gid" '
    /^HOST_UID=/ { print "HOST_UID=" uid; next }
    /^HOST_GID=/ { print "HOST_GID=" gid; next }
    { print }
  ' .env > "$tmp"
  mv "$tmp" .env
fi

docker compose -f compose.yaml -f compose.nvidia.yaml up -d --build

echo
echo "NVIDIA stack started. First startup downloads Docling and Ollama models."
echo "Check readiness with: docker compose -f compose.yaml -f compose.nvidia.yaml ps"
echo "Agent API: http://localhost:8100"


===== FILE: scripts/install.ps1 =====

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$Root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
Set-Location $Root

if (-not (Test-Path ".env")) {
    Copy-Item ".env.example" ".env"
}

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path "outputs" | Out-Null

docker compose -f compose.yaml up -d --build

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Stack started. First startup downloads Docling and Ollama models."
Write-Host "Agent API: http://localhost:8100"


===== FILE: scripts/install.sh =====

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$ROOT_DIR"

if ! docker version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "Docker is not available. Install Docker Engine or Docker Desktop first." >&2
  exit 1
fi

if ! docker compose version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "Docker Compose v2 is required." >&2
  exit 1
fi

if [[ ! -f .env ]]; then
  cp .env.example .env
fi

mkdir -p outputs

if command -v id >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v awk >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  uid="$(id -u)"
  gid="$(id -g)"
  tmp="$(mktemp)"
  awk -v uid="$uid" -v gid="$gid" '
    /^HOST_UID=/ { print "HOST_UID=" uid; next }
    /^HOST_GID=/ { print "HOST_GID=" gid; next }
    { print }
  ' .env > "$tmp"
  mv "$tmp" .env
fi

docker compose -f compose.yaml up -d --build

echo
echo "Stack started. First startup downloads Docling models and ${OLLAMA_MODEL:-the configured Ollama model}."
echo "Check readiness with: docker compose -f compose.yaml ps"
echo "Agent API: http://localhost:${AGENT_PORT:-8100}"

===== FILE: tools/.env.example =====

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Project / ports
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=pdf-extraction
API_BIND_HOST=0.0.0.0
API_PORT=8090
GROBID_BIND_HOST=127.0.0.1
GROBID_PORT=8086
GROBID_ADMIN_PORT=8085
ASSETS_HOST_DIR=./data/assets

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# API
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
APP_NAME=Scientific PDF Extraction API
APP_VERSION=0.2.0
LOG_LEVEL=INFO
MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB=100
SERVE_ASSETS=true
ASSETS_URL_PREFIX=/assets

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Docling - accurate defaults
# CPU mode is the default in docker-compose.yml.
# docker-compose.gpu.yml overrides the device/backend for NVIDIA CUDA.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCLING_PRELOAD_ON_STARTUP=true
DOCLING_MAX_NUM_PAGES=300
DOCLING_MAX_CONCURRENCY=1
DOCLING_DOCUMENT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=1800
DOCLING_ACCELERATOR_DEVICE=cpu
DOCLING_ACCELERATOR_NUM_THREADS=4
DOCLING_OCR_BACKEND=onnxruntime
DOCLING_DO_OCR=true
DOCLING_OCR_FORCE_FULL_PAGE=false
DOCLING_OCR_BITMAP_AREA_THRESHOLD=0.05
DOCLING_DO_TABLE_STRUCTURE=true
DOCLING_TABLE_MODE=accurate
DOCLING_TABLE_DO_CELL_MATCHING=true
DOCLING_DO_FORMULA_ENRICHMENT=true
DOCLING_GENERATE_PAGE_IMAGES=true
DOCLING_GENERATE_PICTURE_IMAGES=true
DOCLING_IMAGES_SCALE=2.0
DOCLING_EXPORT_PICTURES=true
DOCLING_EXPORT_TABLES=true

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GROBID
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
GROBID_BASE_URL=http://grobid:8070
GROBID_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=300
GROBID_MAX_RETRIES=2
GROBID_TEI_COORDINATES=persName,figure,ref,biblStruct,formula,s,p,title,affiliation,note

# Optional image names/tags
EXTRACTOR_CPU_IMAGE=pdf-extractor-api:cpu-local
EXTRACTOR_GPU_IMAGE=pdf-extractor-api:gpu-cu126-local
GROBID_IMAGE=grobid/grobid:0.8.2

# PyTorch builds used by the Docker image.
PYTORCH_VERSION=2.11.0
TORCHVISION_VERSION=0.26.0
PYTORCH_CPU_INDEX_URL=https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
# CUDA 12.6 is intentional: it supports Pascal GPUs such as GTX 1080 (CC 6.1)
# as well as newer NVIDIA architectures in the tested PyTorch wheel.
PYTORCH_CUDA_INDEX_URL=https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu126
